"Unfortunately, he's a little too old, or he might make a good match.

"Well, I plan on inviting him to our ball this weekend."


Chapter 759: First Dance

Saturday night, eight o'clock.

Klein rode his high-end carriage and arrived at 39 Boklund Street --Member of Parliament Macht's house--in two and a half minutes After stealing a glance at the lit fountain that was sloshing with water, he buttoned his tailcoat and walked down the carriage before strolling to the house's main entrance.

Richardson held an exquisitely packaged bottle of Southville red wine and followed closely behind his employer.

After walking through the main door, Klein instantly saw Member of Parliament Macht and his wife, Riana, walk over to welcome him.

The former was wearing an olive-green army officer uniform with an orange-red sash around his waist and a few medals hanging from his chest. In the Loen Kingdom, serving and retired officers enjoyed wearing their military uniforms at balls.

The latter was wearing a yellow long dress with frilly sides and rarely-seen, exquisite lace, making her look different from unmarried ladies. However, she also partially exposed her fair neck and her shoulders.

Klein took the bottle of Southville red wine from Richardson's hand and handed it to Member of Parliament Macht before saying with a bow, "Sorry, I'm late by a few minutes."

This was actually a common situation at Loen balls. Guests would rather be late by a certain amount of time than be early. This was because the masters of the house might still be busy with the final preparations with the ball. It was the worst time for them to entertain guests, but of course, one had to ensure that they weren't late by more than ten minutes.

If Wahana hadn't specially taught him this, Klein would have definitely come early as a form of respect.

"It's fine. The ball hasn't officially begun." Macht glanced at the Southville red wine as he handed it to his valet before nodding with a smile.

At Loen's high society engagements, one had to bring a gift for the master if it was their first time attending a ball. Alcoholic beverages were the most welcomed, but one had to keep in mind that the first gift had to be something locally produced.

After greeting Macht, Klein looked toward Ma'am Riana and saw that she had slightly raised her right hand. Hence, he took one step forward and lifted her palm and bent his back to kiss it.

"You illuminate the entire ball."

Before the ball began, complimenting the masters of the house was a necessary step in Loen social events. And unlike Intis, Loen's hand-kissing etiquette required the lady to gesture that it was possible before a gentleman could do the kiss; otherwise, it would be a serious faux pas.

"Likewise for your arrival," Ma'am Riana replied with a smile.

Then, the couple led Dwayne Dantes through the corridor and into the main hall where a pleasant tune was echoing.

After taking a few steps forward, Maury Macht pointed at a lady in a sky-blue dress.

"My daughter, Hazel."

Klein looked at the girl as his pupils suddenly constricted!

He knew this girl!

To be precise, he had seen her image before!

Back when he had asked Arrodes where he could obtain a mystical item which could steal the Beyonder powers of others, the magic mirror had indicated a scene of an arrogant lady loitering in the sewers, and she was none other than Hazel Macht. She was a lady with wavy black-green hair and bright brown eyes!

She has a mystical item that corresponds to a Prometheus? With her family conditions, why would she be loitering in the sewers? Is it some fortuitous encounter of hers? Was she searching for something or waiting for something in the sewers? She has already become a Beyonder? How did she become one? Could it be that she has a Grandpa parasitizing in her body? Klein, who was donning the "skin" of Dwayne Dantes, instantly thought of many questions, but he pressed his hand to his chest as he bowed with an unperturbed look.

"Good evening, Miss Hazel."

During this process, he glanced at Hazel Macht's face and discovered that she wore a composed look. There was an arrogance in her eyes, and all she did was smile politely and answer, "Good evening, Mr. Dantes."

She didn't have any abnormal reaction, which means that she's unable to sense the aura of the gray fog... At the very least, there's no Grandpa parasitizing her. I can't be certain for now, and I'll have to continue observing... Klein stood up straight as he took a cup of pale-gold champagne from a waiter's tray. He then began chatting with Member of Parliament Maury Macht.

"I never expected you to be a major."

He could tell from the epaulet on Macht's shoulder.

If he was a colonel, Klein would have even suspected if the gentleman was also a Beyonder, but it was hard to tell for a major.

"Haha, it's nothing. There are many opportunities to render meritorious services in Balam," Maury answered. "Of course, the weather there is especially unsuitable. I've always been suggesting to the army's higher-ups to design a uniform for West Balam and to get rid of the traditional dark colors; otherwise, the officers will only feel as if they are beef waiting to be roasted."

As for the enlisted soldiers, most of them wore red tops and white bottoms.

"Yes, the weather there is completely different from what it's like in the country. Even Desi Bay isn't that hot." Klein indicated that he had been to the Southern Continent, and he had been to either East or West Balam to corroborate his hunting experiences that he had mentioned a few days ago.

After a few minutes of small talk, Macht apologized and walked to the staircase with his wife. At the second floor, he raised a cup of red wine while standing by the railings facing the main door and said, "Thank you everyone for coming to our ball. First, let us toast the deities. They are the source of everything beautiful."

He and Ma'am Riana tapped four times on their chests as they softly praised the Goddess. The other guests also praised the deities they believed in via their own means.

Macht continued having his cup raised as he said with a smile, "Second, a toast to the kingdom. It is a stable bedrock."

"To the kingdom." Klein raised his cup of champagne and spoke with the other guests around him.

Afterward, Macht surveyed the area and asked humorously, "Finally, what shall we toast to?"

Klein's mind whirred as he said loudly with a smile, "A toast to the improvement of the air in Backlund."

Macht was taken aback as he could not help but smile in response.

"Excellent. That's a great suggestion.

"A toast to the improvement of Backlund's air. This is a symbol of us living better lives. Cheers!"

The fixing of the atmospheric pollution issue had always been one of his political ideals as a House of Commons Member of Parliament. He had ultimately been pushing for the relevant bills, and he had played a significant role in the improvement of the environment. Therefore, toasting to the improvement in Backlund's air was equivalent to toasting to himself. It was more euphemistic and more aboveboard.

All the guests echoed in a spirited burst as they finished the drinks in their hands.

Immediately afterward, Member of Parliament Macht held Ma'am Riana's hand, and they went down to the hall. They then started the opening dance in the mellow music.

All the gentlemen present began finding their first partners to dance. Klein took another cup of champagne as he leisurely studied the guests.

Hmm, Ma'am Mary is here as well... He swept his gaze and found someone familiar. As one of the major shareholders of the Coim Company, with a wealth amounting to tens of thousands, Ma'am Mary had formerly hired Sherlock Moriarty to investigate her ex-husband's act of adultery.

She's a member of the National Atmospheric Pollution Council, so it's normal that she has a close relationship with the Member of Parliament... Klein didn't attempt to invite her to a dance, since he here as Dwayne Dantes. He didn't know this lady who lived in another street.

He looked away and looked elsewhere. He saw Hazel Macht holding a cup of white wine and standing to the side. She wore a clear smile of alienation as she watched the gentlemen lock onto their targets to invite them for a dance.

This lady actually looks pretty good. She's dignified and pretty.

She should have been the star at this ball, with people yearning to invite her to a dance. However, the way she exudes that look of arrogance, looking down at people with a supercilious look, makes any gentleman who casts his eyes on her shift over to another target.

I've seen this look in the eyes of certain Beyonders as well. They no longer think of themselves as mortal, and they often have a sense of superiority when facing ordinary folks... Heh heh, this implies that Miss Hazel is likely a Beyonder... That's right, if she isn't a Beyonder, how would she dare to loiter in the sewers... She's from the Marauder pathway? But how is she to act as a Marauder or Swindler with such arrogance? It's hard to imagine... Seeing that the hosts were almost done with the opening dance, he began seriously considering who he could invite.

Dwayne Dantes is in his early forties, so it isn't appropriate to invite a lady for his first dance, unless it's someone confirmed to be a junior. And the first dance of most ma'am's would be done with their husbands... Hmm, I should be able to invite people I'm familiar with or the hosts... Klein swept the dance floor and found the only lady Dwayne Dantes was familiar with. It was his etiquette teacher, Wahana.

Invite her? No, she likely already knows that I've secretly helped her. To invite her for the first dance can easily make her misunderstand. It might even affect her relationship with her husband and incur unnecessary trouble for Dwayne Dantes... It's not like II'm Emperor Roselle who has a penchant for the wives of others. No, he has a penchant for everything. In short, I should avoid creating gossip... Klein shifted his gaze and heard the music change. It went from a mellow melody to something brisk.

It was a piece of countryside music that was popular in central Loen. It was well-liked by nobles and was often used for the first dance.

With the change in music, the gentlemen walked to the ladies and madams they had selected. Klein also noticed that no one approached Hazel Macht.

She's one of the hosts of this ball... Besides, I can observe her at a close distance... Heh heh, if she really is a Beyonder of the Marauder pathway, it implies that the gray fog is able to "converge" Beyonders from the neighboring pathways of the Seer pathway... Klein wore a gentle smile as he unhurriedly walked to the arrogant lady.

"Miss Hazel, may I have the pleasure of a dance with you?" Dwayne Dantes with his white sideburns gave a standard bow as he said.

Hazel glanced at him and, after a few seconds of silence, said, "It will be my honor."

She then extended her palm.

Klein politely held her hand and entered the dance floor as they began a brisk and lively dance.

Glancing at her beautiful but deadpan face, Klein said with a smile as he tried probing, "I just noticed that many young gentlemen wished to invite you to a dance, but they were unable to muster their courage."

Hazel looked up and swept her gaze at him and said, "Mr. Dantes, that's not a polite topic."

"..."

Klein choked, lost as to how to reply.


Chapter 760: What a Small Circle

I thought she would contemptuously say that she doesn't like immature and incapable men, hinting that she isn't impressed with ordinary people. Who knew she can't even be bothered to answer this question... Heh, this sense of superiority will easily lead to a loss of control for subsequent advancements... Klein could not help but lampoon inwardly.

From what he knew, Beyonders were only humans who had additional powers. It was equivalent to having plenty of money or status. In fact, they were still considered human and had no way of escaping human society. Only by reaching Sequence 4 would one experience a qualitative change.

Furthermore, most demigods continue being active in human society. Even the Sequence 1 Snake of Fate Will Auceptin is being an obedient unborn fetus... Perhaps only at the level of Blasphemer Amon and the others will they be able to view the real world as a "god"... Klein's mind whirred as he said, "I'm sorry. I was once a merchant who often spent time in the Northern Continent and Southern Continent, and I have had little experience with a ball. Heh heh, I mean, this type of ball."

"It's fine," Hazel replied calmly as though she didn't care about the topic he had just raised.

If it were anyone else, they would have been at a loss for a conversation with this arrogant lady. All they could do was focus on the dance, but Klein was considered quite a knowledgeable and experienced person at this point. He knew quite a bit of the different Beyonders in the mysterious world, so with his apology, he said with a smile, "This is a challenge that isn't simpler than the sea. It similarly has beautiful scenery but hides countless difficulties. Of course, the sea also has stories of all kinds of treasures. Some of them are clearly fake, but others sound rather realistic but are impossible to verify. It's just like Death's Key that's ranked first amongst them."

"Death's Key?" Hazel looked up at Mr. Dwayne Dantes who was a lot taller than her.

Indeed, a Beyonder with a strong sense of superiority would often have their interest piqued when it's something that involves mystery... Klein chuckled to himself and he nodded gently.

"Yes, it's rumored to be hidden somewhere in the Berserk Sea..."

He used the legends he had heard back when he was aboard the White Agate, as he added more of the details he had heard during his career as an adventurer.

During this process, he couldn't avoid mentioning the Four Kings and the Seven Pirate Admirals.

Hazel was clearly interested in these as she responded to Klein in a rare instance. She would even occasionally ask other questions, making the dance between them less awkward. Without realizing it, their dance came to an end.

Klein skillfully ended the topic and switched to asking, "Do you plan on returning to where you were, or do you plan on heading over to get some food?"

After a dance, the gentleman had to abide by the lady's wishes and send her to where she wished to go. It didn't have to be where she originally stood.

Hazel opened her mouth as though she wished to ask further, but she ultimately didn't speak further. She nodded her head in a reserved manner, "Where I was."

Hehe, she clearly misses the stories at sea... She's just a big brat. As long as you grasp her temper and find what interests her, she's actually not difficult to interact with... Klein held back his smile as he sent Hazel back to the periphery of the dance floor where she previously stood.

As for him, he acted casual as he walked to the long table that had all kinds of food placed on it. He picked up a plate and began to scoop a serving of pan-fried Dragon-Bone Fish, and he matched it with some sliced black pepper steak.

Compared to the dance and entertaining others, food is the true essence of a gathering... As Klein thought, he worked hard at trying to make his appearance while eating appear elegant enough.

At that moment, he saw Ma'am Mary walk over and fork a piece of foie gras soaked in red wine onto her plate.

When Klein saw her glance at him, he politely smiled with a nod as a response.

"How may I address you? I haven't met you before at the balls and banquets hosted by Member of Parliament Macht." Perhaps it was because of Dwayne Dantes's gray sideburns and deep blue eyes that were very charming, the ordinary-looking Ma'am Mary with slightly high cheekbones took the initiative to ask.

Klein laughed and replied, "I'm a merchant who just returned from Desi Bay, Dwayne Dantes. I live on this street.

"Ma'am, do I have the honor of knowing your name?"

Mary nodded in thought, and she roughly understood this man to be a merchant who was trying hard to enter high society, just like she was previously.

She said with a smile, "Mary Schott, Coim Company's executive director."

She didn't mention that she was the biggest shareholder of the Coim Company, or mention that she was a member of the National Atmospheric Pollution Council. This was a Loen-styled euphemism.

Mary Schott. She has taken up her original last name? Right, she has already divorced... Klein silently thought to himself and said with a smile, "I know of this company. Its main business is in anthracite and high-quality coal. It has expanded rapidly in the past few months. Heh heh, to be frank, I intend to invest in it, but I don't seem capable of competing with the rest."

After the atmospheric bills were passed, there was a drastic increase in the demand of anthracite and high-quality coal. Coim Company managed to develop itself in ways that exceeded its past efforts. Its overall valuation had already exceeded 250,000 pounds. Klein wasn't shooting his mouth when talking about investing, but that he believed that this industry would become even more important in the coming years until humanity found a resource to replace it.

Mary had always been very proud of the National Atmospheric Pollution report she pushed for, as well as the development of the Coim Company, so she could not help but smile when she heard that.

"This is because people are beginning to pay attention to the environment that they are living in."

Having said that, she gently sighed and said, "As it gets better, trouble also increases as a result."

Having "just" acquainted himself, Klein didn't ask about the trouble. With his prior acquaintance with Ma'am Mary, he easily found a topic of interest and had a good conversation with her.

Heh heh, her attitude toward Sherlock Moriarty and Dwayne Dantes is very different... Despite being someone she knows, just a change in looks and identity will be given a brand new form of treatment without any problems arising. This feeling is truly magical... As they chatted, Klein felt wistful as he felt that the additional Faceless potion he had consumed was quickly digesting.

After a few minutes, a handsome man with bright blond hair walked over with a cup of red wine. He smiled at Ma'am Mary and said, "Mary, what are you talking about?"

"Hibbert, this is Mr. Dwayne Dantes from Desi. His experiences at sea and West Balam are truly interesting," Mary immediately introduced the two. "Dwayne, this is Mr. Hibbert Hall, the eldest son of the Earl of East Chester. Heh heh, we should be calling him Lord, but he prefers people to address him as Mr. Chief Secretary. He's the chief secretary of the National Atmospheric Pollution Council."

I've heard you mention him before. Of course, that was when the identity, Sherlock Moriarty, was still active... The Earl of East Chester is a major noble in the nation. He's considered the top brass when it comes to high society... Klein politely bowed without appearing overly low.

"Please permit me to convey my thanks as an ordinary citizen. The work of the National Atmospheric Pollution Council has allowed us to live in better living environments."

Hibbert Hall was rather pleased with such sincere gratitude, so he smiled in reply.

"This is all thanks to the hard work all of us have put in."

By the side, Mary said with a smile, "Dwayne, don't mention such matters again. You will make Hibbert arrogant. No, I was just joking. He's more humble than all the noble children I know. He should be having a vacation in the East Chester County's fief at this time and spending his time hunting with his friends, but he immediately returned after I sent him a telegram informing him that I was invited to this ball by Member of Parliament Macht."

"It's not only for this ball. There are many things that require my attention. My father, Earl Hall, would also frequently commute between Backlund and our fief before June," Hibbert seriously explained.

A gentleman who places great importance on his social image... Klein made a preliminary judgment.

When Mary heard that, she asked in passing, "Is there anything still keeping you back? When are you leaving Backlund?"

"Most of my work has already been completed. There's only one matter left. Heh heh, my sister, Audrey, is very interested in the Backlund Bike Company's 10% shares. She hired a specialized team to help her in the negotiations, and I'm responsible for overseeing the matter," Hibbert said without much thought.

Backlund Bike Company's 10% shares? What a coincidence... I have to say that the circle of high-society is quite small after all... Klein sighed to himself as he deliberately mentioned, "I also found a team to attempt to purchase the 10% shares, but I only managed to offer up to 9,000 pounds. Unfortunately, I wasn't able to compete with the other competitors and could only give up."

Hibbert glanced at him with a look of surprise.

"You have good taste."

He didn't mention how much his side's bid was, to prevent his competitor from participating again.

9,000 pounds... Mary silently muttered, realizing she had underestimated Dwayne Dantes's wealth.

At that moment, the music for the third dance sounded. Hibbert Hall turned to Mary and said, "May I have the pleasure of a dance with you?"

"That's what I've been waiting for." Mary immediately extended her hand.

This made Klein unable to exchange name cards with them; however, he wasn't in a rush, because it was still some time before the ball ended.

After getting another plate of food, he enjoyed it while looking at the dance floor, admiring the madams' and ladies' dancing.

During this process, he noticed that Member of Parliament Macht and Ma'am Riana were mingling with different guests from time to time, having happy conversations with them and even dancing with them.

According to Walter, after confirming the guest list, the hosts need to seriously conclude every guest's preference and background, so as to tailor a different topic of conversation or jokes for them. This is to make everyone feel as though they are being treated uniquely... Socializing in high society sure is troublesome... Heh heh, this might be why Loenese gentlemen tend to have receding hairlines... Klein lampooned as he sighed in reflection.

He looked away and looked at his cleared plate. He seriously considered if he should invite another lady or madam to a dance, or if he should eat a little more.

At that moment, he caught Hazel Macht's figure through the corner of his eye. She was heading for the third story with hurried footsteps.

Chapter 761: Good People and Good Deeds

What happened? Klein instantly tensed.

He had experienced too many accidents in the past, and he knew how easily he became entangled in Beyonder affairs. Whenever he encountered anything similar, he couldn't help but go on alert. It resembled a form of post-traumatic stress disorder.

After watching Hazel Macht's figure disappear up the staircase, Klein sensed that she was merely in a hurry, not panicking.

That means she has the matter under control... Besides, Macht is a Member of Parliament in the House of Commons, a member of high society ranked just below the kingdom's nobles. There should be Beyonders protecting him. Yes, Earl Hall's eldest son is here too, so his bodyguards must be Beyonders as well... And Saint Samuel Cathedral is only a ten-minute carriage ride from Boklund Street. If anything truly happened, the Nighthawks, priests, and bishops would rush over... Unless someone had resolved to sacrifice themselves, no one would cause an incident at this ball... Klein gradually relaxed and formed another guess about Hazel's situation.

She was rushing to the third floor to deal with the negative effects of a mystical item!

The question Klein had previously asked Arrodes was where he could obtain a mystical item capable of stealing another person's Beyonder powers, and one of the answers he had received was Hazel Macht!

Thinking back to their dance, Klein rapidly reconstructed Hazel's image in his mind, focusing on her various accessories.

Hair ornaments, earrings, necklace, brooch, fishnet gloves... Which could it be? Klein withdrew his gaze and realized he was thirsty. He picked up a glass of water and drained it.

Just as he set down the glass, he saw his etiquette teacher, Wahana Heisen, approaching with a plate.

The lady wore a red dress, yet didn't look tawdry. She smiled at Dwayne Dantes and said, "I noticed that you don't enjoy drinking alcohol."

"I once missed an important engagement because of drinking." Klein casually added another detail to Dwayne Dantes's persona, making him seem experienced and profound.

Of course, he knew when to exercise restraint. He didn't use his Faceless powers to remove a finger and prove how resolute he had been when making that former oath.

Wahana smiled thoughtfully upon hearing this.

"Your past is shrouded in mystery. Many young ladies find that irresistibly attractive."

She didn't continue the subject. Instead, she said, "I forgot to tell you that the problem my husband faced has been resolved."

Klein picked up a glass of champagne and raised it. Smiling, he said, "That is certainly cause for celebration. Congratulations."

He made no mention of the help he had secretly provided.

Wahana gave him a long, penetrating look as she raised the glass of red wine in her hand.

"Cheers."

After clinking glasses and taking a sip, Klein politely excused himself, set down his glass, and headed for the washroom.

It wasn't because he wanted to go above the gray fog, but solely because of Death Knell's negative effects. He had drunk too much water and needed to relieve himself.

When he emerged from the washroom, Klein glanced up at the staircase leading from the second floor to the third. He saw Hazel Macht descending at an unhurried pace, her expression perfectly composed.

Indeed, it wasn't a serious problem... It was likely caused by the negative effects of her mystical item... I wonder what it is... Klein sighed in relief and casually swept his gaze across the dance floor. During the interval between two songs, he walked over to a lady and invited her to dance.

Given Dwayne Dantes's appearance and bearing, his invitation was naturally accepted.

And so, with dancing, eating, conversation, and more eating, the ball slowly drew to a close as the guests bid their farewells one after another.

Having accomplished his objective of exchanging calling cards, Klein did the same. He was neither the first nor the last to leave.

The hall soon fell silent. Ma'am Riana supervised the servants as they cleaned the room, then beckoned to her daughter, Hazel Macht.

"Mr. Dwayne Dantes performed better than I imagined. Several ladies even asked me about him in private," Ma'am Riana said obliquely. "Hazel, what impression did you form while dancing and conversing with him? You're far more mature than other girls your age. I trust your taste and judgment."

She knew her daughter well and had deliberately added the final sentence. Otherwise, Hazel was unlikely to offer a detailed answer.

Hazel wasn't quite so arrogant when facing her mother. After some thought, she said, "He isn't very familiar with this circle and readily brings up subjects that might cause offense, but he's very knowledgeable."

"Very knowledgeable..." Ma'am Riana repeated in mild surprise.

From what she knew of Hazel, that was high praise indeed.

She couldn't help worrying that her daughter had taken a liking to Dwayne Dantes.

Hazel thinks little of the eligible bachelors around her because they're too young, inexperienced, and incapable? Dwayne Dantes happens to be precisely the kind of man an unusually mature girl would like... Riana suddenly regretted inviting the gentleman to the ball.

She knew that, with Hazel's temperament, the girl might very well elope if anyone opposed her newfound love.

Hazel seemed to discern her mother's thoughts and said without emotion, "I only like men who are sufficiently powerful."

Phew... Riana silently sighed in relief, no longer concerned about the previous possibility. Hazel was a girl who considered lying beneath her dignity.

Late that night, Hazel rose from bed. With the aid of her night vision, she changed into clothing that allowed easy movement.

She climbed down from her bedroom balcony, carefully avoided her family's bodyguards, crossed the garden, and reached the middle of Boklund Street. Not every sewer manhole was wide enough for a person or fitted with a vertical metal ladder.

Hazel deftly shifted the manhole cover aside, climbed down, and closed it from below.

Nearly forty-five minutes later, she moved the cover again and emerged into the shadows of the street.

At that moment, Hazel saw a shadow nimbly vault into a nearby garden.

Number 160... She read the corresponding address.

It was none other than Dwayne Dantes's residence.

On the manor's third floor, Klein was once again awakened by his spiritual intuition. He had the urge to capture the intruder who had disturbed his sleep and feed them to Creeping Hunger.

This time, he directly opened his iron cigar case and released his Wraith marionette.

Senor, dressed in his dark red coat, first stepped into the full-length mirror before leaping to the oriel window in his valet's room.

"He" observed Richardson and saw the valet sit up, staring at the door with fear and anxiety.

The door silently opened, and a shadow flashed inside.

Under the crimson moonlight, the intruder's brownish-yellow skin, soft features, and short, curly black hair became visible. He clearly hailed from the Southern Continent.

Exuding a gloomy, cold aura, he stood by the door and looked at Richardson. In a deep voice, he said, "Have you decided?

"Do not believe you can attain a peaceful life merely by leaving. The blood of Death's subjects flows within you. You are destined to give up everything to restore God's glory.

"Think of your deceased mother. Think of the insults you once suffered. Do you want your child to grow up beneath the contemptuous gazes of others and remain a servant forever?"

"But what can I do..." Richardson lowered his head, forcing out the words with great difficulty.

"Wait for the mission." The intruder's voice softened.

Richardson gave no firm answer, seemingly locked in an inward struggle.

The intruder didn't appear to care about his hesitation. Treating it as agreement, he turned, left the room, and retraced his steps.

Death's subject... Someone from the Numinous Episcopate, or another organization trying to restore Balam? Having witnessed everything, Klein leaned back against his bed and silently wondered, What mission will they give Richardson? Steal my money to fund the organization? Or cause a terrorist incident at one of high society's balls?

By then, the intruder had climbed down from the balcony, crossed the garden, and vaulted over the perimeter fence of steel bars.

Suddenly, he saw a figure pounce from his left. He dodged, clenched his fist, and threw a punch.

Thud!

The punch struck the black figure but passed straight through it, as though he had hit a shadow cast by a streetlamp.

At the same moment, a heavy blow landed on the back of his head, and he collapsed unconscious.

Hazel's figure immediately emerged behind the intruder, excitement written across her face. She looked as though she had just pulled off a successful scam.

She quickly reined in her emotions and resumed her haughty demeanor. Then she turned toward the black iron gates of 160 Boklund Street.

The girl bent over, seized the intruder by the arm, and dragged him to Dwayne Dantes's doorstep.

Immediately afterward, Hazel released him, erased any traces, walked forward with her chin slightly raised, and pulled the doorbell.

Then she swiftly departed, making straight for her home through the street's shadows.

On a streetlamp outside Number 160, a piece of glass set into the black iron gate reflected a figure in a dark red coat and an old tricorne hat. It had witnessed the entire process.

How should I deal with this... Inside his room, Klein was stumped.

He knew that Hazel had anonymously done her neighbor a good deed by subduing an intruder, but if his butler reported the incident to the police, it would be investigated in detail and eventually transferred to the Nighthawks. Then the identity of whoever had knocked the intruder unconscious would become an important question.

If Klein were truly an ordinary person, it wouldn't have mattered--he could simply let the Nighthawks conduct their investigation. However, not only was he a powerful Sequence 5 Beyonder, he was also plotting to steal an item from behind Chanis Gate. He didn't want any unforeseen complications to ruin his plans, forcing him to change identities yet again.

Frankly, his first idea had been to find a way to dismiss Richardson.

However, what he had just heard changed his mind somewhat.

If I dismiss Richardson, it will be the same as pushing him into the abyss despite his desire for a quiet life. It will force him to associate with those people... Unfortunately, Dwayne Dantes has a "mission"; otherwise, helping him in passing wouldn't be difficult... Klein sighed inwardly.

Ten seconds later, the unconscious intruder abruptly stood, cracked his neck, and hid in the nearby shadows. At that moment, Butler Walter emerged from the house's main entrance after hearing the doorbell.

Chapter 762: Nation Reestablishment Society

At the gates, Walter peered through the gaps by the light of the streetlamps and found no one outside. The street was silent.

For an instant, Walter wondered if he had misheard and the doorbell hadn't rung at all!

Composing himself, he quickly walked to the servants' quarters at the rear of the property. He woke several servants and had them carry double-barreled hunting rifles as they began patrolling the main building, guarding against any bandits or burglars who might try to break in.

Walter didn't contact the police immediately because nothing had actually happened. The earlier ring might merely have been a tramp's prank.

Meanwhile, in the nearby sewers, the intruder gripped the metal rungs as he slowly descended into the unlit depths.

He soon stopped, leaned against a moss-covered wall, and slowly slid down until he sat on the filthy ground.

His eyes closed again as though he were still unconscious. Before him, a middle-aged man in a dark red coat and an old tricorne hat instantly appeared. It was none other than Klein's marionette, Wraith Senor.

Senor bent down and searched the intruder's pockets, finding 7 soli and 11 pence, along with tiny cloth bags containing various powders.

In his room, Klein controlled his marionette remotely from dozens of meters away. As he identified the powders, he confirmed his theory: they were all herbal powders of the Death domain, and some could be used for spirit channeling!

The intruder was very likely a Beyonder of the Corpse Collector pathway from Balam. Even if he hadn't reached Sequence 7, Spirit Medium, it was perfectly normal for him to carry the corresponding herbal powders, essential oils, and extracts. After all, these materials weren't used solely for spirit channeling.

Klein immediately controlled Senor and had him establish a ritual to pray to The Fool.

Then he went above the gray fog to respond, allowing Senor to complete the remaining steps.

Afterward, he returned to the real world and continued controlling Senor, directing him to channel the intruder's spirit.

Passing through a storm of glimmers, Klein saw the man's spirit. It appeared listless, blurred, and translucent.

"What is your name? Which faction do you belong to?" Senor asked in a deep voice.

The intruder answered blankly, "Godotpos. I belong to the Black Skeleton Gang."

The Black Skeleton Gang. I believe it's active near the border between East Borough and the docks, close to the Backlund Bridge area. Its members are mainly of Balam heritage. Although they aren't as barbaric and boorish as the Zmanger Gang, they're no strangers to violence... Recalling the intelligence he had previously gathered, Klein had Senor continue the interrogation.

"What is your main business? Why are you looking for Richardson?"

In a daze, Godotpos answered, "We fight for God.

"We were originally members of the Balam Nation Reestablishment Society. We founded the Black Skeleton Gang to gather intelligence and obtain funds. Apart from that, we have another mission: to seek out any items connected to Death and send them back to the Southern Continent.

"This time, we received verified information that Earl Wolf's collection contains a mask taken from the Eggers family mausoleum. That family descends from God.

"To obtain the mask, we need to send someone into Earl Wolf's household as a servant, or infiltrate one of the balls or banquets he hosts. Richardson is an excellent choice. He has no history with any other organization, and he's an experienced servant."

Noble households often "inherit" their servants. Infiltrating one clearly isn't easy... Only when they suddenly need considerable manpower will they hire short-term workers...

Come to think of it, that situation really does occur. At today's ball, several ladies mentioned that some financially strained nobles sell much of their land and many of their manors. They also dismiss nearly all their servants, retaining fewer than ten to maintain a barely respectable way of life. When they hold large balls or banquets requiring more manpower, they spend money to hire a group of temporary workers from the Family Servant Assistance Association and keep up appearances...

Also, Earl Wolf actually owns a mask from the family descended from Death. I recall Mr. Azik's surname is Eggers... Unfortunately, I don't want any accidents disturbing me at present. Otherwise, I might have devised a way to help Mr. Azik obtain that mask... Klein muttered inwardly and made Senor continue.

"How do you know Richardson?"

Godotpos replied blankly, "We met at a manor in East Balam. We were both slaves then.

"Among the slaves were people who secretly spread the faith of Death. Living as we did, Richardson, his mother, and I couldn't help becoming believers in Death. We secretly joined an organization with considerable influence among the slaves there.

"Later, Richardson's mother died of an illness and he was taken to Backlund, while I remained in East Balam until I found an opportunity to escape.

"Several years later, I was sent to Backlund and happened upon Richardson. H-he had actually forgotten his mother's death and the abuse he'd endured. He forgot his faith in God and allowed his will to be eroded by what he called a peaceful life!

"To avoid me, he deliberately made mistakes and kept changing employers, but how could he have guessed that his former companion was no longer an ordinary human?"

Everyone has the right to choose, as long as they don't harm others. But Richardson and I are very different people... In his room, Klein closed his eyes and made Senor ask in a deep voice, "What is the organization with such influence among the slaves?"

Godotpos hesitated before saying, "The Eternal Life Society. Those who believe in Death will receive eternal life in the Underworld once they leave this real world of pain and sorrow."

The Eternal Life Society... I know of it. It's a branch of the Numinous Episcopate... As a former Nighthawk, Klein knew a great deal about such matters.

He continued controlling Senor and interrogating Godotpos, obtaining extensive information about the Eternal Life Society, the East Balam Nation Reestablishment Society, and the Black Skeleton Gang. He also confirmed that Godotpos and his gang had innocent blood on their hands.

After completing the spirit channeling and removing all traces of it, Klein waited thirty minutes before letting the Wraith enter Godotpos's body. He controlled the man as he climbed out of the sewers and returned to the shadows of the streets.

By then, the servants at 160 Boklund Street, armed with double-barreled hunting rifles, were no longer as vigilant during their patrols. They seemed to believe any danger had passed.

Klein pretended not to notice anything and continued sleeping in his master bedroom. However, he had already set up a ritual to summon and respond to himself. Carrying Azik's copper whistle, the iron cigar case, and Creeping Hunger, he silently left his residence in spirit form.

He followed Godotpos while constantly maintaining a distance of eighty meters. Using his marionette to possess this "hostage," he had the man circle around to another street and board a rental carriage waiting by the roadside.

About an hour later, Godotpos returned to the Black Skeleton Gang's headquarters, a small house near the docks.

Numerous firearms were stashed there, and several operatives dispatched by the East Balam Nation Reestablishment Society formed the upper ranks of the Black Skeleton Gang.

Following the agreed method, "Godotpos" knocked on the door and told the member who approached him, "Richardson has submitted."

"Very good." The member cast Godotpos a cursory glance, stepped aside, and let him pass.

"Godotpos" surveyed the house and found high-yield explosives and a pile of rifles stacked in one corner. Several senior members of the Black Skeleton Gang were gathered together, discussing something.

"Godotpos, want a smoke?" The earlier member offered him a cigarette.

It was a cigarette made to Southern Continent tastes, fashioned from dried tobacco leaves mixed with small quantities of herbs.

Godotpos accepted the cigarette, casually picked up a box of matches from the table, removed several, and lit them.

Then he tossed the burning matches into the corner where the highly flammable explosives lay.

"..."

Everyone present stared at Godotpos in stupefaction, momentarily unable to comprehend what had happened.

Rumble!

Seated on a public bench dozens of meters away, Klein remained still as flames blazed behind him and a blast of hot air surged from the house.

A few seconds later, Senor appeared beside him in his dark red coat, bearing signs of burns.

The Wraith placed a hand over his chest and bowed before returning to the gold coin inside the iron cigar case.

Unfortunately, I can't collect the Beyonder characteristics. Otherwise, it wouldn't look like an accident... It would certainly be suspicious if none of the Black Skeleton Gang's upper echelon were Beyonders... Klein sighed inwardly. After erasing all traces, he ended the summoning and returned above the gray fog.

The next morning, he rose and washed as though nothing had happened, then waited for his valet to bring a change of clothes.

Richardson entered silently and completed his work with practiced skill.

Afterward, he stepped back and bowed his head.

"Sir, after serving you through this week, I wish to resign."

He usually received his weekly salary from Housekeeper Taneja.

"Why?" Klein asked, looking at himself in the mirror as he adjusted his waistcoat.

Meanwhile, he thought leisurely, Not bad. You know to resign of your own accord rather than bring trouble to your employer...

Richardson had already prepared an excuse.

"I believe I lack the ability required of a valet. At last night's ball, while interacting with the other guests' servants, I realized how deficient I was."

Klein smiled.

"Everyone begins without experience. Few are born with it. Think it over again and give me your final answer tomorrow."

"Yes, sir." Richardson said nothing further. He took the initiative to leave the room and went down to the first floor to collect his employer's morning papers.

As usual, he first leafed through them and placed the most interesting articles on top.

While doing so, his gaze froze on one report:

"An explosion occurred at 79 Dirham Street in the Backlund Bridge area. It is suspected to be connected to the Black Skeleton Gang...

"According to the police, all senior members of the Black Skeleton Gang died in the accident, including Lima, Moreira, and Godotpos..."

This... Richardson shook his head, suspecting that he was dreaming.

Chapter 763: Mr. X

At 160 Boklund Street, in the second-floor dining room, Klein had just bitten into the buttered toast served by a servant when he saw his butler enter.

Walter bowed and said, "Sir, someone of unknown identity rang our doorbell last night.

"As it was already very late, I didn't wake you. Instead, I had the servants patrol the premises with double-barreled hunting rifles.

"With your permission, I'll visit the nearby police station and ask them to increase their nighttime patrols along this street."

Aside from the expense, employing such a butler has no drawbacks... Klein nodded slightly and took a drink of the freshly delivered milk.

"Very good."

At Number 39 Boklund Street, the home of Member of Parliament Macht, Hazel entered the second-floor sitting room and saw her mother, Riana, speaking with the housekeeper.

"What happened?" She tucked a lock of black-green hair behind her ear.

Riana chuckled. "Someone rang Dantes's doorbell late last night."

"A prank?" Hazel sat down.

"No one knows. In any case, Dantes's butler made a special trip to the police station this morning," Riana said, recounting what she had learned from a servant.

Hazel nodded slightly. "It's best to involve the police."

"But it's useless. No one knows who rang the bell. Apparently, there was no one there." Riana laughed and shook her head.

Hazel was taken aback and blurted, "There was no one there?"

"That's right. Dantes's butler seems only to have asked the police to strengthen the nighttime patrols along our street. That's good news." As the wife of a Member of Parliament in the House of Commons and the daughter of a famous lawyer, Riana enjoyed excellent relations with the police.

"Not a single person..." Hazel repeated softly before falling silent.

After a while, she left the sitting room and returned to the third floor.

Along the way, she couldn't help clenching and swinging her fist as if testing something. A look of obvious puzzlement furrowed her brow.

In his bedroom, Klein looked at the huge pile of gold coins before him. All he could see was the gleam of gold!

There were 4,000 pounds in gold coins!

After a week spent gathering funds, Admiral of Stars Cattleya had finally completed the transaction for the Scales of Luck. In addition to the gold coins, she had paid another 6,500 pounds in banknotes.

To be honest, a pile of gold coins is far more visually impressive than an equivalent stack of banknotes... Sighing, Klein took the 13 Loen gold coins he had received from Senor out of his pocket and tossed them onto the pile.

After doing so, he took out the adventurer's harmonica and blew into it.

Reinette Tinekerr silently appeared before him, carrying her four blonde, red-eyed heads in one hand.

"Ma'am, this is the first installment, a total of 4,013 pounds." Klein tore his gaze away from the mound of gold and looked at Miss Messenger.

He was actually curious about how Miss Messenger would move such a large quantity of gold. He remembered that she always collected letters with her teeth.

"Very good..." "You still owe..." "Five thousand..." "Nine hundred..." "Eighty..." "Seven..." Reinette Tinekerr's heads said in turn.

There's no need to remind me... Klein forced a smile. "I'll try to gather the remainder as soon as possible."

Reinette Tinekerr said nothing more. One of her heads opened its mouth wide with apparent effort.

Suddenly, the space before her became dark and profound. All the gold coins were drawn into vortex-like surging water.

Within seconds, the enormous pile of gold vanished.

Reinette Tinekerr's four heads nodded simultaneously before she returned to the spirit world.

I still have 8,156 pounds and 5 Loen gold coins... I can barely be considered a tycoon... I could actually afford to take advantage of any investment opportunities that appear, rather than merely driving up the price and mentioning them in passing... That way, no one will suspect me of fraud for the time being... Heh heh, be optimistic. Perhaps an investment will let me earn back what I spent. Money always earns money quickly... Klein silently exhaled and looked out the window, forcing himself to admire the thinly fogged streets.

At night in East Borough, the surroundings were pitch-black. There were few streetlamps, and many of those were broken. It seemed as though countless monsters and criminals lurked in the darkness.

Wearing a hooded cloak, Xio Derecha turned into a narrow alley and arrived outside what appeared to be a dilapidated house.

This was the new East Borough gathering she had previously mentioned to Fors!

Xio wasn't in a hurry to knock using the agreed signal. She first looked down to inspect her attire.

Unlike her usual clothing, she wore a pair of tall boots.

They didn't look unusual, but Xio knew their soles were extremely thick. She had stuffed all manner of things inside, allowing her to "magically" appear taller.

This effectively concealed Xio's most distinctive characteristic!

Reassured by the presence of her triangular blade, Xio pulled up her hood and knocked on the door with the agreed signal.

She was soon led into a sitting room, where she chose a seat at random.

Once most of the participants had arrived, the gathering's host finally entered.

He was of medium build and about 1.75 meters tall. He wore a classical black robe, a pointed mage's hat, and a brass mask. He exuded an ancient and mysterious air.

His skin is rather dark, but not exactly brown. He resembles someone from Feynapotter or Desi Bay... But I can't be certain. It could simply be a tan... His curly hair is raven-black. That does support my first guess... Xio appraised him with a Sheriff's eye.

The brass-masked man looked around the room before giving a low laugh.

"You may call me Mr. X."

Mr. X... The corners of Xio's mouth twitched.

Since becoming an outer member of MI9, she had learned a fair amount about secret organizations, including the Aurora Order.

Therefore, she knew very well that the Aurora Order's Oracles used letters as code names and liked to style themselves Mr. or Ma'am.

In her view, introducing themselves in that fashion openly revealed their identity and background. After all, most of the members attending these gatherings were Beyonders starved for knowledge!

Isn't he afraid someone will report him? No wonder the gentleman from MI9 said the Aurora Order is full of lunatics whose behavior can't be understood through ordinary logic... Since Mr. A disappeared, several Backlund gatherings have been led by impostors or by former associates exploiting his name for their own profit. The hosts were quickly reported and arrested... Hmm, Mr. X might not necessarily belong to the Aurora Order. Perhaps he's simply like the others... Xio withdrew her scrutinizing gaze and silently watched the gathering continue.

She seldom reported such matters. Having lived as an unaffiliated Beyonder, she knew how difficult it was to survive with such an identity. As long as no incident occurred, she wouldn't give MI9 such intelligence.

If he's confirmed to be an Oracle of the Aurora Order, I'll report him. Those people are all lunatics. They'll bring terrible danger... As Xio considered the matter, she watched the others exchange information and complete transactions.

She participated very little, partly because there was no information or item that interested her, and partly because she was saving for her Interrogator potion.

After the gathering ended, she returned to her home in Cherwood Borough. Xio found Fors reclining at ease, reading with a face mask said to moisturize the skin.

"How was it? How was the gathering?" Fors asked, pretending indifference.

Xio tossed her cloak aside. "There were quite a few participants and items, but most were intended for Low-Sequence Beyonders."

"No one offers anything good at the first gathering." Fors lowered the novel in her hands.

"Yes." Xio nodded. "The organizer calls himself Mr. X, but no one knows whether he's connected to Mr. A."

Mr. X... A suspected Aurora Order Oracle... Could he be Lewis Wien? Fors's interest sharpened. She languidly leaned back and asked, "What does he look like?"

"He wore a mask!" Xio rolled her eyes. "Do you think I can see through solid objects?"

"No, no. I mean his build." Thanks to her teacher, Dorian Gray, Fors had seen Lewis Wien's complete figure and knew his physical characteristics.

As a Sheriff, Xio easily reconstructed Mr. X's appearance. She then asked in puzzlement, "Whether or not he's connected to Mr. A, isn't he worried someone will report him for using such a name?"

After hearing Xio's description and seeing the portrait she produced with her Sheriff powers, Fors was delighted. There was a high probability that Mr. X was the Abraham family's traitor, Lewis Wien!

He isn't afraid of being reported because he's a Traveler. He can escape calmly no matter what happens? If the official organizations use the same methods they employed against Mr. A, they truly won't be able to restrain him... Fors muttered inwardly. She stopped discussing the subject and changed the topic.

Once Xio went to bathe, Fors hurriedly used her Astrologer powers to question her spirituality. She superimposed Lewis Wien's image over Mr. X's and received confirmation that they were the same person!

It really is him! Fors sprang to her feet and paced the sitting room in excitement and delight.

Her first thought was to report him to the major Churches and include a warning that the target possessed a Traveler's powers. Then she remembered her prior attempt to hire Mr. World to assassinate Lewis Wien.

Whatever else, I should first ask Mr. World whether he's free to accept the mission. I can't afford to offend him. Remembering everything Mr. World had done, Fors couldn't help shuddering.

Having made up her mind, she first confirmed that Xio was bathing and wouldn't emerge anytime soon, then prayed to Mr. Fool.

"...Please tell Mr. World that Lewis Wien, an Oracle of the Aurora Order, has appeared. There is a high probability that he is a Traveler, and he uses the code name 'Mr. X.'"

Chapter 764: First Investigation

Klein was returning from Saint Samuel Cathedral to Boklund Street in a four-wheeled carriage when he heard the overlapping, illusory pleas.

A woman... It isn't urgent... After making a rough judgment, he didn't immediately go above the gray fog to respond.

Sweeping his gaze across the streetlamps dispelling the darkness, Klein looked away and raised the gold-rimmed white porcelain cup to take a sip.

Beside him, Richardson noticed this and, after mustering his courage, said, "Sir, I've thought it over. You were right. Everyone begins without experience. Few are born with it. Thank you for giving me this opportunity to grow."

After confirming that Godotpos and his gang were dead, he had finally relaxed enough to consider his professional future.

Changing employers repeatedly within a short period was a stain on a servant's record. Once he left Dwayne Dantes's employ, Richardson believed it would be difficult for him to find another position as a valet.

That would be devastating.

Not only was a valet's annual salary at least 25 pounds, far better than that of any nonmanagerial position and comparable to a lady's maid, but serving as a valet also provided the greatest opportunity to become a butler!

By accompanying their male or female employers, attending to every trivial concern, and acting as their representative and assistant, valets developed their skills. They could acquire every quality a butler required and become their employer's confidant. Given an opportunity, they could readily become a land steward, assistant butler, or deputy butler, gradually advancing until they became a butler themselves.

Richardson yearned for a peaceful life, but that didn't mean he was willing to remain a servant forever. Without question, he hoped to rely on hard work to earn more and improve his standing. Becoming the butler of a wealthy household was his ultimate ambition.

"It isn't too late to realize that now," Klein replied with a smile, agreeing to retain Richardson.

After returning to 160 Boklund Street, he instructed Housekeeper Taneja to prepare supper for half past eleven. Then he went up to the third floor, removed his coat, and entered the bathroom.

A maid had already filled the bathtub with water heated to the proper temperature five minutes earlier.

Klein wasn't in a hurry to bathe. He went above the gray fog to determine who had prayed to him.

Mr. X... Traveler... Miss Magician is quite efficient... Without realizing it, she has matured considerably... Klein mused.

After serious consideration, he conjured The World Gehrman Sparrow and had the false figure pray amid the veil of gray fog.

"...Give me the time and location, and provide more information. Only then will I act."

Klein's reasoning was simple. He found a Traveler's powers quite useful, but Dwayne Dantes couldn't be absent from Boklund Street for an extended period or remain shut in his room all the time. If Miss Magician could provide detailed, reliable intelligence, allowing him to carry out a single assassination, that would be fine. But if she expected The World to gather the information piecemeal, there was no way he could do it without disrupting his own plans.

Fors soon replied.

"I'll try to gather it as quickly as possible."

Since the time and location of Mr. X's next gathering hadn't been confirmed, all she could do was wait patiently.

After settling the matter, Klein returned to the real world, undressed, and lowered himself into the bathtub.

Warm water enveloped him. He closed his eyes in comfort, feeling the fatigue plaguing his body and mind wash away little by little.

Recently, he had visited Saint Samuel Cathedral several times to listen to Bishop Elektra explain The Revelation of Evernight. He had learned the appearances and characteristics of two more Keepers, but hadn't seen any of them more than once, preventing him from determining their rotation.

And these actions will bring trouble of their own. Klein opened his eyes, looked at the steam above him, and sighed inwardly.

The first investigation should begin soon...

Someone who frequently entered the interior of Saint Samuel Cathedral would likely be investigated by the Nighthawks. With his origins still uncertain, such an investigation was almost inevitable.

If they don't investigate, it would be a serious dereliction of the Nighthawks' duty... Klein slowly exhaled.

In the basement of Saint Samuel Cathedral, Leonard slowly emerged from a quiet room.

His green eyes held a trace of strange black water as countless illusory bubbles and ripples appeared and disappeared.

"Not bad. You're already a Soul Assurer and have nearly caught up with me." Standing in the corridor, Daly Simone congratulated him with self-deprecating humor.

She still wore her hooded black robe, blue eye shadow, and rouge. She possessed a strange, uncanny beauty.

Looking at this familiar woman, Leonard found her bearing even colder than before. Countless shadows seemed to hide around her in layers, descending into frigid depths.

"Clearly, I'm still far behind you. In your present condition, you should be ready to advance to Gatekeeper, right?" Leonard didn't behave too casually with Daly and spoke with relative formality. If he attempted a joke, he would inevitably be the one left blushing with embarrassment, not her.

Gatekeeper was Sequence 5 of the Death pathway.

"I was ready two months ago," Daly said without concealment, her expression twisting slightly.

Leonard roughly understood her meaning and nodded.

"You haven't accumulated enough merit?"

Daly immediately curled her lips.

"That's right.

"It's like I'm already in bed with everything prepared, only to discover there aren't any condoms in the house. Worst of all, it's late at night and most of the neighborhood shops have closed!"

What an extraordinary analogy... Leonard judged it wiser not to respond. Smiling, he said, "You could choose to handle certain cases."

Without giving Daly a chance to speak, he pointed toward the far end of the corridor.

"Captain Soest is still waiting for my advancement report."

Daly said nothing as she watched him leave.

By the time his back vanished around the corner, the woman's expression had grown distant. She murmured, "Captain Soest..."

In the room temporarily assigned to Leonard's Red Gloves team, Soest, who had just become a Spirit Warlock, saw his indolent subordinate enter and casually tossed him a dossier.

"Very good. You've advanced. I'll congratulate you later. First, investigate this target's dream."

It was already late at night, but to the Nighthawks, whose ranks consisted mainly of Sleepless, it was no different from daytime. Indeed, they felt stronger at night.

"Isn't this the local Nighthawks' responsibility?" Leonard caught the dossier and asked casually.

"They've recently been overwhelmed with cases and are short of manpower. They requested our assistance," Soest explained indifferently.

Leonard asked no more and looked down, leafing through the dossier.

The first thing he saw was a photograph, and the middle-aged gentleman depicted there had left a deep impression on him!

Dwayne Dantes... Leonard's pupils immediately contracted.

He knew this man. He knew the person was an undying monster who had survived since the Fourth Epoch. He was at least a Saint, perhaps even stronger!

Furthermore, he knows my secret and Old Man's identity... Leonard instinctively raised a hand to rub his temples.

"Captain Soest, I've only just advanced, and my spirituality is a little unstable."

"Is that so..." Only then did Soest realize he might have made a mistake. He hurriedly looked toward another Red Glove. "Albert, you do it."

Albert was a man in his thirties with pale blond hair and pallid skin. He didn't look particularly healthy.

Leonard sighed in relief and handed him the dossier.

At that moment, his heart suddenly jolted. Would Albert suffer some harmful effect if he entered the undying monster's dream?

He immediately felt a trace of regret, believing he should have done it himself. At least he understood the degree of danger and had faced the man before. He wouldn't inadvertently provoke him.

Dwayne Dantes probably won't do anything to Albert... If he overreacts and causes anything abnormal to happen to Albert, he'll immediately expose himself to us. And given our Church's power and the situation in Backlund, there's no way he could leave the city alive... Leonard quickly calmed down, believing that Dwayne Dantes would adopt a gentler method of evading the dream investigation.

He pulled over a chair and sat beside Albert. Though he appeared to be reading the newspaper absentmindedly, he watched Albert constantly to prevent any accident.

At 160 Boklund Street, in the master bedroom, Klein abruptly became lucid within his dream, aware that "someone" had arrived.

The Nighthawks' investigation? he wondered. Looking around, he found himself in a half-open room.

Then he heard a knock on the door.

"Come in..." Klein tried to make his voice sound like a dreamy murmur.

The doorknob turned and the door opened. A slender blond man in a black trench coat entered. It was none other than the Red Glove Albert.

"I'm a superintendent with the Backlund Police Department." Albert casually displayed his identification and sat opposite Klein.

"How may I help you, Officer?" Klein slipped into character.

He knew that, under a Nightmare's influence, he needed to appear natural.

Albert conjured a stack of papers and softly read, "Dwayne Dantes. Male. Hails from Desi County..."

He recited all the intelligence they had gathered and asked, "Is this information accurate?"

"Partly, but some of it is false," Klein replied "honestly."

The only true parts are probably "male" and "single"... he mocked himself inwardly.

Pleased to have made progress so quickly, Albert asked with a deadpan expression, "Which parts are false?"

Klein had prepared for this. Pretending to recall, he said, "Most of my wealth didn't come from mining, but from adventuring in the Southern Continent."

Drawing on Anderson's description of West Balam, he fabricated the story of how a commoner had amassed his fortune through eloquence, intelligence, experience, and courage in a region where conflict between Loen and Intis frequently erupted.

The story wasn't particularly detailed and consisted mainly of an outline. Its chief purpose was to convince the Nighthawks that Dwayne Dantes wasn't a Beyonder, but an ordinary man with a taste for adventure and risk. Such rags-to-riches stories were common in Loen.

Chapter 765: Monday Again

When Klein finished, Albert asked several targeted questions based on his account to ensure the details were consistent.

Without question, he received satisfactory answers.

"Thank you for your cooperation. Pleasant dreams." Albert smiled, rose, and bowed. Then he used his Nightmare powers to influence Dwayne Dantes once more. When the man awakened, he would retain a vague impression of having had such a dream but be unable to recall the details.

After doing so, Albert walked to the door, turned the handle, and left the dream.

Indeed, the Nighthawks place too much faith in Nightmare powers. If I were leading this investigation, I would design a series of questions that approached the matter from different angles, then cross-reference the answers to uncover any inconsistencies... Heh, the best method would be to enlist Miss Justice's help in creating a professional set of psychological questionnaires and have the target complete them in the dream. If he were faking anything, contradictions would inevitably emerge between his psychological state and the image he wished to project across the different assessments. That is, unless he were also a psychological expert capable of identifying each question's true purpose... Klein leaned back on the sofa and looked out the window.

In the darkness, the streetlamps' glow appeared dim and pale, illuminating the surroundings with gloomy silence.

Klein watched silently for several seconds before curling his lips into a self-deprecating smile.

In the basement of Saint Samuel Cathedral, Leonard first sighed with relief when he saw Albert awaken normally and heard his report. He felt even greater fear toward the undying monster from the Fourth Epoch.

In the City of Silver, lightning streaked across the sky, illuminating every street.

Carrying the Axe of Hurricane, Derrick Berg left his home and headed for the twin towers north of the city.

Along the way, he encountered several City of Silver residents. Some were busy taking their children to general education lessons, while others patrolled every corner in groups. Their task was to ensure that anyone who died in an accident at home without a relative present wouldn't become an evil spirit before someone could end their life.

The presence of these people made the City of Silver feel alive, and from time to time Derrick heard the laughter and cheers of young children.

He couldn't help remembering the days in Afternoon Town. Each day, he had seen only about twenty people, and most of the time they had been confined to a fortified building. Outside, monsters lurked in houses beneath the cover of darkness. They were eradicated again and again, yet always returned from unknown places. Every member of the expedition felt helpless, as if true safety were impossible. They could never rest easy, always needing to be ready to go all out. There was no opportunity to relax.

No ordinary creature could endure such prolonged stress and vigilance. For that reason, the City of Silver had long ago developed a rotation system.

The first expedition team hadn't needed much time to return from Afternoon Town to the City of Silver, but the mandatory quarantine and recuperation period couldn't be avoided. Only today had Derrick finally adjusted to a mental state he believed could withstand the negative effects of advancement.

He had previously reported to Chief Colin Iliad that he had obtained the Notary potion formula, and had been permitted to exchange this discovery for the corresponding Beyonder ingredient--the feathers of a Spirit Pact Bird.

As for the items he owed The Moon, he had acquired them while patrolling the outskirts of Afternoon Town and sent them over with Mr. Fool's help.

After advancing, I'll be qualified to choose a mystical item intended for Beyonders below the High Sequences... Derrick quickened his pace with a trace of anticipation and soon reached the twin towers.

Although the material warehouse and mystical items were all kept in the spire under the six-member council's supervision, Derrick's destination was the steeple, where he could exchange contribution points for items.

Just as he was about to enter, his spiritual perception stirred. He instinctively looked up at the spire and saw a woman in a black robe patterned with purple standing behind a window, gazing down at him.

She had silver-gray hair, light gray eyes, and a beautiful face. She was none other than one of the Elders of the six-member council, Shepherd Lovia!

When their eyes met, Lovia's gaze seemed to pierce his soul, but her expression remained unchanged. She even nodded slightly as though greeting him.

She isn't greeting me, but the person behind me... Derrick suddenly understood.

This insight came from the experience he had slowly accumulated under the Tarot Club's guidance.

He nodded in response, withdrew his gaze without revealing anything unusual, and entered the steeple at an unhurried pace.

At night, in a private Bayam harbor, the Golden Dream lay moored beside the quay, its strange main cannon positioned amidships.

Carrying the local specialties given to him by the Resistance, Danitz waved to them with a radiant smile while climbing the gangway to the deck.

He had enjoyed an exceptionally comfortable life recently. As an envoy delivering weapons, food, and small quantities of Beyonder ingredients, he had received excellent treatment. He either feasted sumptuously or amused himself by hunting and boasting. He had even been invited to witness a ritual in which the Sea God blessed "His" believers.

After witnessing all this, he had suddenly realized that Bayam--perhaps all the colonies--would inevitably become embroiled in fierce conflict. It wasn't something that would subside within decades, perhaps not even within a century.

Therefore, Danitz decided to sell most of his properties in Bayam, retaining only one. He would then find an opportunity to purchase property in Trier, the capital of Intis, and Backlund, the capital of Loen, as well as in some idyllic, peaceful villages.

I can also take the opportunity to return to Elema Town and visit the old man and Mom. Yes, I can buy one fewer house and get them a vineyard... Danitz enthusiastically waved to the Resistance once more.

Then he puffed out his chest and smugly asked Flowery Bow Tie Jodeson, "Where's Captain? I need to report the latest developments."

Jodeson tsked contemptuously.

"She's obviously in the captain's cabin."

Meanwhile, he lampooned inwardly, This fellow has grown more and more arrogant since establishing ties with Gehrman Sparrow! But that mad adventurer truly is terrifying. He actually hunted down Admiral of Blood!

"Heh!" Danitz scoffed. Adopting a provocative swagger, he entered the cabin and saw Vice Admiral Iceberg Edwina Edwards.

His provocative air instantly vanished, replaced by a smile.

"Captain, I've completed the mission."

"Details." Edwina set down the book in her hands.

Danitz had come prepared. He gave a detailed account of recent events, exaggerating his own importance. When he finished, he said, "Captain, I met Gehrman Sparrow. He asked me to inquire whether there have been any abnormalities involving Elven Songster Siatas's corpse and that golden wine cup."

Edwina didn't answer directly. She walked to a corner of the captain's cabin, where a black wooden chest stood.

The Golden Dream was preparing to sail for Sonia Island, so Siatas's and Mobet's remains were still aboard the ship. Edwina had stored them in a specially prepared chest.

Lowering herself onto one knee, Edwina opened the wooden chest and exposed the intertwined remains to the light.

The badly crushed golden wine cup rested silently in a bony palm without displaying any abnormalities.

"There is nothing abnormal," Edwina concluded.

Danitz glanced over and memorized her answer, preparing to report it to the mighty Fool once no one else was present, so that "He" could relay the message to that lunatic, Gehrman Sparrow.

Nothing abnormal? Above the gray fog, Klein frowned slightly, feeling both puzzled and relieved.

According to his theory, there was a high probability that the golden cup had a problem. That nothing unusual had occurred exceeded his expectations, yet it was also the answer he wanted. He didn't wish Siatas's and Mobet's eternal slumber to be disturbed.

Perhaps it requires some additional catalyst? Heh heh, let's hope that never happens... Klein muttered to himself before looking toward the junk pile where Groselle's Travels lay.

Because he had no immediate intention of entering the sea of collective subconscious and was currently focused on the Antigonus family's notebook, he had postponed his second search of the book's world.

Phew... Klein exhaled, withdrew his gaze, and prepared for that week's Tarot Gathering.

At three in the afternoon, Backlund time, dark red beams rose on both sides of the long, mottled table, materializing into indistinct figures.

As usual, Audrey was in a good mood--perhaps even better than usual. Her brother, Hibbert Hall, had sent a telegram informing her that the purchase of a ten-percent stake in the Backlund Bike Company had been completed for a total of 12,000 pounds.

Furthermore, she didn't need to rush back to Backlund to sign any documents. Before Hibbert departed, she had signed a letter of authorization in the presence of two lawyers. All she needed to do now was wait for the matter to conclude and sign a confirmation letter for her brother.

Audrey's lips curved upward as she rose and addressed the figure seated at the head of the bronze table, concealed by gray fog.

"Good afternoon, Mr. Fool~"

May Mr. Fool bless me. I hope there will be clues to the fruit of an Illusory Chime Tree today... Immediately afterward, she began praying silently.

Once greetings had been exchanged and everyone was seated, Cattleya didn't disappoint Klein. She once again lowered her head, not daring to look directly at him.
