Has Queen Mystic still not discovered why Emperor Roselle was assassinated... She's still passing diary pages through Ma'am Hermit... What a pity. I've been at the family fief recently and haven't had much contact with the Psychology Alchemists. I can't obtain any new diary pages... Hmm, I'll visit the Relic Search and Preservation Foundation in a few days. Perhaps they've found something... Audrey listened with curiosity.

The Fool Klein chuckled.

"Very good.

"You may consider your request."

In truth, I know Queen Mystic has already given you the question... I wonder what she's doing in Backlund... Klein's thoughts began to wander.

Cattleya soon conjured the three diary pages and handed them to Mr. Fool.

Chapter 766: The Deities' Anchor

Knowing that the diary pages specially selected by Queen Mystic contained important information, The Fool Klein focused on the yellowish-brown goatskin in his hands.

"11th September. Ever since I became an angel, I've felt my identity dissociating. In my heart, my soul, and the depths of my mind, a voice constantly urges and influences me, engendering uncontrollable coldness, bloodlust, cruelty, and madness.

"This doesn't come from the outside world or the influence of a god from the same pathway. I can clearly sense that it comes from one's genes, from humanity's collective subconscious passed down through generation after generation. It stems from the Beyonder characteristic itself, not its remnant psychological influences.

"It fills me with a powerful urge to hunt and kill, to devour every living being around me that possesses Beyonder characteristics. Resisting it takes tremendous effort. Even though I've acted and digested the potion, the condition shows no sign of improving.

"No wonder Mr. Door said rationality is temporary, but madness is eternal."

"28th September. I haven't written a diary entry in ages. Over the past half month, I seemed to watch myself being replaced by a stranger. Bit by bit, I became cold and terrifying. Even my daughter Bernadette could awaken only the faintest trace of fatherly love in me. Very, very little.

"Just as I was about to go mad, I seemed to hear countless praises. They came from my subjects, from those who benefited from my reforms. They were believers who regarded me as the Son of Steam. They acclaimed me, erected statues in my image, wrote stories about me, and composed songs and poems in my honor.

"Their voices seemed like a ship's anchor, helping me secure myself in place.

"I began to resist the desire and the roaring within me. Bit by bit, I emerged from it and once again possessed the ordinary feelings of a father, a husband, and a man.

"Sequence 2 alone causes such changes. At Sequence 0, the level of a true god, how terrifying must the struggle against madness be?

"Perhaps 'They' also need an anchor to resist the Beyonder characteristics, the powerful inclination to lose control, and the urges buried deep in the collective subconscious.

"I think I understand why 'They' establish Churches and spread 'Their' faith, why stories are written about the saints of 'Their' factions and legends preserved about 'Their' corresponding angels...

"But why do 'They' have no anthropomorphic form beyond symbols?

"I don't understand.

"I'll ask Mr. Door in the future. 'He' seems to know a great deal about the domain of deities. Had 'He' been released back then, there might well be an additional deity today."

"29th September. After rereading yesterday's entry, I recalled the corresponding rituals for my Sequence 4, Sequence 3, and Sequence 2. They clearly contained elements of madness and cruelty, like those performed by villains in novels.

"A Sequence pathway may well be a road destined for madness and despair.

"Yet it is humanity's only road to extraordinary power.

"How laughable and ironic.

"Do we strive to save ourselves only to destroy ourselves more thoroughly?"

The first page left Klein's heart heavy and oppressed. The Roselle who had written those words was no ordinary person, but an angel, a member of the Twilight Hermit Order, and someone who had seen the Blasphemy Slate. His understanding of the mysterious world and Beyonder characteristics far surpassed Klein's, yet he was even more pessimistic. He seemed to believe the world's origins were inherently distorted and mad, destined for destruction.

However, the seven deities have apparently found a way to preserve "Their" rationality. Ordinary humans aren't useless. Their recognition and combined spirituality can help a deity "anchor" "Their" original image, preserving the memories and rationality "They" accumulated over the years... This can be inferred from Roselle's experience... But why would the seven deities abandon "Their" anthropomorphic images and use abstract Sacred Emblems? That doesn't fit my theory... I don't understand... Wasting no time, Klein turned to the next page.

"5th December. Night of the Blood Moon. I spoke with Mr. Door.

"As always, 'He' asked me to help 'Him' return to the real world, but didn't insist too forcefully. Furthermore, 'He' casually answered some of my questions.

"Heh heh, it's as though 'He' is playing a game and trying to raise my affection for 'Him.' Unfortunately, I'm sorry, but I eliminated that option long ago.

"Since I already knew the legends of the Kings of Angels, I chiefly asked Mr. Door about their level of power, knowing I had nothing to lose by asking.

"The word 'accommodated' is peculiar. I asked about it, but Mr. Door didn't answer directly. 'He' merely said that, before performing the ritual to advance to Sequence 0, a Sequence 1 angel unable to 'accommodate' the Uniqueness would find it a burden rather than an aid.

"Hmm, that makes sense. It's akin to using a Grade 0 Sealed Artifact. The negative effects are often terrifying, and a Uniqueness must be even more extreme.

"I also asked which Kings of Angels had 'accommodated' their Uniquenesses, and again Mr. Door didn't answer directly. 'He' only said that Amon and Adam were the envy of all angels because 'They' were born with a Uniqueness and never needed to consider 'accommodation.' From another perspective, does that mean Amon and Adam were born in a state equivalent to having consumed a Sequence 1 potion and the Uniqueness? As expected of the Creator's sons!

"That Creator, also known as the ancient sun god, was actually powerful enough to pass a Uniqueness and Sequence 1 Beyonder characteristics to each of 'His' two sons... Was 'He' trying to purify 'Himself' and eliminate unnecessary interference?

"Then has Mr. Door also 'accommodated' a Uniqueness, and perhaps even consumed two Sequence 1 potions? I didn't ask because I knew 'He' wouldn't answer.

"During our conversation, Mr. Door warned me not to say Adam's full name directly, or it would be detected and our conversation discovered.

"I had an inkling why. Laughing, I asked whether 'He' hadn't just said Adam's full name Himself.

"Mr. Door said it didn't matter. Sequence 4 of the Apprentice pathway is Secrets Sorcerer, which carries the meaning of maintaining secrecy. Though inferior to the Evernight pathway's Servant of Concealment, it was enough to block detection at 'His' level.

"I asked several more questions about the deities, but Mr. Door didn't answer. 'He' only said that, when I possessed the ability and opportunity, I could go above the moon and take a look. I would then understand many things.

"This somewhat accords with some of my previous thoughts, but I suspect 'He' is luring me there to seize an opportunity to return to the real world. After all, every one of 'His' appearances is connected to the moon!"

Whenever Mr. Door appears, there's a wealth of information, usually enough to fill an entire page... Hmm, his explanation of the Kings of Angels' power matches my theory...

In its broadest sense, King of Angels refers to a quasi-deity who, through various means, surpasses Sequence 1 without reaching Sequence 0. This includes accommodating the Uniqueness or consuming additional Sequence 1 potions. In its narrower sense, King of Angels refers to the eight kings among all angels ruled by the ancient sun god, the Creator worshiped by the City of Silver. Of course, "They" must also satisfy the broader definition... Thoughts flashed rapidly through Klein's mind.

He strongly agreed with Roselle's theory about the ancient sun god. He believed the Creator worshiped by the City of Silver had reclaimed too many authorities from the ancient gods, causing "Him" to show signs of chaos and insanity. Therefore, "He" had decisively sired two sons to eliminate some of the "waste."

Simply put, Amon and Adam are the complimentary gifts that came with drinking the potions... From the looks of it, Angel of Imagination Adam clearly possesses the Spectator pathway's Uniqueness. "He" is likely the mysterious leader of the Twilight Hermit Order. Since ancient times, "He" has been interfering with the course of history to revive "His" father... I wonder whether "He" has advanced to Sequence 0... Even if "He" hasn't, the number of angels the Twilight Hermit Order can mobilize likely exceeds my imagination... Oh, Secrets Sorcerer carries the meaning of secrecy as well as concealment... Klein instantly recalled the symbol on the back of The Fool's chair.

It was the Pupil-less Eye representing secrecy and the Contorted Lines representing change!

He quickly reined in his thoughts and turned to the third diary page.

"28th November. I dreamed of Grimm again.

"He was the cleverest of my subordinates, but regrettably, he died in the Fog Sea from an unknown infection while exploring that nameless island. He didn't even leave a child behind.

"Back then, I knew that nameless island concealed unimaginable secrets and dangers, but lacking sufficient strength, I could only restrain myself.

"This dream is probably my spirituality reminding me that I can now explore the island, uncover its secrets, and resolve Grimm's matter once and for all.

"29th November. I summoned three subordinates and, with Benjamin Abraham's help and some searching, finally found the nameless island again.

"I didn't enter immediately, deciding instead to rest for a day at its edge.

"Edwards said he also often dreams of Grimm and feels deeply guilty for failing to save him then.

"'It wasn't your responsibility, but mine.' That was what I told Edwards, because I was their leader."

"30th November. We ventured deep into the island.

"Beyonder creatures that extensive records claim are extinct still existed there. They gathered without conflict, as though consecrating something...

"This group of unintelligent Beyonder creatures appeared to be conducting a ritual!

"Were they praying to an unknown deity?

"During the ritual, I saw Grimm..."

Chapter 767: Passing a Message

Grimm, who was infected on the nameless island and unquestionably died, appeared on that same island again? Emperor, when did you begin writing horror stories? And where's the rest? Klein's gaze froze on the third diary page's final line, but there was no further information.

He was similarly alarmed by Roselle's description of the Beyonder creatures' strange gathering and their worship of an unknown entity. Not all Beyonder creatures possessed intelligence or could be communicated with. Many were monsters that had lost control or were innately mad.

And these out-of-control monsters had gathered to worship an unknown existence!

Unfortunately, the Emperor didn't record the nameless island's coordinates... Even if he had, I wouldn't dare go there for fear of infection. If I died only to revive on the island, that would be troublesome... I'll need to be at least Sequence 4, perhaps Sequence 3, before I'm capable of exploring it... As Klein thought, the diary vanished from his hands. He looked at Cattleya and asked with a smile, "What is your request?"

Without hesitation, Cattleya asked, "Honorable Mr. Fool, I wish to know whether Emperor Roselle once participated in an extremely secretive, ancient organization."

Extremely secretive and ancient... The Twilight Hermit Order? Emperor Roselle was once a member? Audrey immediately recalled Mr. Fool's previous mention of the organization.

Alger also recalled the relevant matter. In Miss Justice's account of Backlund's Desire Apostle case, she had mentioned a mysterious organization. Mr. Fool had warned the members not to utter its name in the outside world because "any mention of it will be known!"

Such an organization does suit Emperor Roselle's status... Alger nodded inwardly.

Klein thought of something else.

Queen Mystic's selection of diary pages is vague. She can't ask questions precisely based on their contents. That means she only knows that entries from certain periods contain key information, or she can discern Roselle's emotions when he wrote them.

Ma'am Hermit's question posed no difficulty. Klein smiled. "Yes."

At the same time, he silently warned himself, In the future, I must remember that Queen Mystic's questions might be entirely unrelated to the pages she provides... Fortunately, I've read quite a few of Roselle's diary entries and understand the Emperor reasonably well...

Then he added with a smile, "It is your turn."

He didn't directly name the Twilight Hermit Order, hoping to answer another question on the subject in the future.

Cattleya bowed in gratitude, then looked at The Magician.

"I wish to obtain clues about the Abraham family's direct descendants. One thousand pounds."

She felt no burden in naming the price, as Queen Mystic would certainly reimburse her.

Why did she look directly at me? Does she already know my teacher belongs to the Abraham family? Fors was alarmed at first, then realized it wasn't surprising. Apart from Ma'am Hermit, this wasn't a secret to the Tarot Club's other members. It was perfectly normal for them to mention it after completing a transaction with her.

Although 1,000 pounds was extremely tempting, Fors had no intention of selling information about her teacher. Only a handful of people in her life had treated her with genuine sincerity, and she treasured those relationships.

After some deliberation, Fors asked, "Why are you looking for them?

"If you provide a reason, I can relay the message. Any further developments will depend on them. I have no say in the matter."

Indeed, she may appear ordinary, but she conceals many secrets. She's a cautious and meticulous woman... Cattleya sighed inwardly and nodded. "That's very reasonable.

"I'm searching for the Abraham family's direct descendants in the hope of obtaining information about Mr. Door. Pass this message to them and ask whether cooperation is possible.

"For merely relaying the message, I'll pay you 350 pounds. If they're willing to accept the matter, I'll pay another 650 pounds."

Fors restrained herself for a full second before answering, "Deal."

Only then did she grasp the implication behind Ma'am Hermit's words.

Mr. Door is connected to the Abraham family? That's right. My bracelet came from Ma'am Aulisa, and it came from the Abraham family... Yes, Teacher still doesn't know that I'm aware he belongs to the Abraham family... In my letter, I can only mention casually that someone at a gathering was searching for the Abraham family's direct descendants in connection with Mr. Door... I wonder how Teacher will react...

At this point, Miss Justice felt happy for her friend, sensing that Fors had escaped the predicament of poverty.

Fors is already Sequence 7. She has nearly caught up with me. I must become a Hypnotist as soon as possible... Thinking this, Audrey glanced around at the Tarot Club members, hoping they had clues to an Illusory Chime Tree's fruit.

Miss Justice's gaze makes it so difficult to refuse... Fors lowered her head in embarrassment. She still had no clues about the fruit in any of her circles.

Seated opposite Audrey, Alger considered for two seconds. "I have a clue, but it will take two weeks to obtain it."

He did have one. Long ago, while exploring the primitive island with Qilangos, he had discovered an Illusory Chime Tree. Lacking sufficient strength, he hadn't approached it. Now that he had the opportunity to advance to Sequence 5, he naturally wouldn't relinquish any chance to earn money. After all, he still owed Admiral of Stars for information about the Obninsks.

By mentioning it now, he was, in a sense, asserting his rights. When the time came, he and The World would explore together. It was best to determine the division of spoils beforehand and avoid conflict.

Of course, when confronting the Illusory Chime Tree, Gehrman Sparrow would likely stand idly aside without lending any help.

"No problem!" Audrey was delighted and didn't even ask the price.

She was already considering finding an excuse to buy it directly from the Psychology Alchemists after returning to Backlund in June.

With both transactions concluded and no other members making requests, Cattleya repeated her search for Mythical Creature blood, while Emlyn again mentioned his hunt for the four remaining believers of the Primordial Moon. The Tarot Gathering then entered the free-exchange segment.

Derrick said naturally, "I've already become a Notary."

You don't have to tell us that... The Hanged Man had the urge to cover his face, but calmly replied, "Although becoming a Notary greatly enhances one's physical attributes, it remains primarily a support role. If the notarization of a power is valid, it will be temporarily strengthened. If declared invalid, the Beyonder power will be forcibly dispelled. A Notary is also skilled at drafting contracts. Once signed and confirmed, even a Sequence 5 cannot violate one. If a Sequence 4 demigod tries to break it by force, they will pay a considerable price..."

As a member of the Church of Storms, knowing the Sun pathway's strengths and weaknesses was fundamental. Alger carefully explained the details to Little Sun. He also advised him to select a mystical item effective at controlling a target. If none was available, he should choose one with formidable offensive power.

"Thank you, Mr. Hanged Man." Derrick thanked him from the bottom of his heart.

Deep down, he regarded Mr. Fool as the greatest and most worthy of reverence in the Tarot Club; Mr. World as the most impressive and the model he should emulate; and wise Mr. Hanged Man as the most reliable and kind.

Emlyn thought, He has actually caught up with me. No, I can't let him surpass me. I must finish the hunting competition as soon as possible and claim the reward!

After considering for a few seconds and receiving Mr. Fool's permission, he conjured the slender Moon Puppet, which resembled a wooden rod.

"Ladies and gentlemen, does anyone know what this is?"

He knew Mr. Fool was certainly aware of the answer, but presently had nothing of equal value to offer this mighty existence. Therefore, he could only rely on the Tarot Club's other members.

Isn't that the puppet I saw at the gathering? Mr. Moon has already found his target... Fors likewise waited curiously for an answer.

Cattleya studied it carefully and frowned.

"It is likely a Moon Envoy.

"It is a Beyonder item created by believers of the Primordial Moon through a bloody ritual spanning centuries. It is said to be imbued with divine power. Every one possesses unimaginable horror.

"Where did you obtain it? It's extremely dangerous. You had best turn it over to the Sanguine's upper echelon."

Emlyn shifted in his seat and chuckled.

"I encountered it while hunting a believer of the Primordial Moon.

"She's dead, while I'm still alive."

Mr. Moon's self-aggrandizement is painfully obvious... Audrey, a senior Spectator, chuckled inwardly.

Cattleya was taken aback. Without trying to conceal her surprise, she asked, "How did you do it?

"Did the Sanguine's upper echelon assist you?"

Emlyn's mouth fell open, and he was briefly at a loss for words.

Only then did he realize this subject wasn't suitable for detailed discussion.

"Ahem." He cleared his throat and looked toward the head of the long bronze table. "I sought Mr. Fool's help."

Chapter 768: "Conversation" Between Smart People

Sought Mr. Fool's help? That's possible? Cattleya was stunned.

After being punished and watching "His" Blessed, Gehrman Sparrow, grow ever more formidable, she no longer doubted Mr. Fool's status or abilities. When Mr. Fool had drawn her in using an ancient object and allowed her to escape the pursuit of knowledge, she had realized "He" was at least an existence on the level of the Hidden Sage or Primordial Moon. For some reason, however, "He" couldn't interfere with the real world, leading her to suspect "Him" of being an ancient god in the process of awakening.

The Moon's words surprised her because they meant Mr. Fool could provide substantive aid to Tarot Club members, rather than merely supplying knowledge or drawing them above the gray fog.

On one hand, this means I can pray directly to Mr. Fool for help when I'm in danger... This is more extraordinary than any ritual... On the other, it reveals that Mr. Fool is recovering faster than I imagined... Countless thoughts flashed through Cattleya's mind.

Audrey, Derrick, and the others weren't surprised. More or less all of them had sought Mr. Fool's help, and they had even witnessed an angel serving this great existence.

Seeing every gaze fall upon him, Emlyn drew in his neck and lifted his chin.

"I paid a price.

"It was a fair and equal exchange!"

A fair and equal exchange... An immediate and effective response from a mighty existence is priceless! Cattleya couldn't help retorting inwardly.

She didn't say so aloud because she also hoped to make use of such a "fair and equal exchange" in the future. It would effectively give her one life more than others!

Fors was puzzled by Mr. Moon's deliberate emphasis on fairness and equality. Everyone who sought Mr. Fool's help made a corresponding payment!

Mr. Moon truly cares about his dignity. No--a Sanguine... Audrey carefully evaluated the emotional changes Emlyn had undergone in those few seconds.

As the conversation continued, the Tarot Gathering gradually ended.

After returning to the City of Silver, Derrick first reviewed the strengths and weaknesses of a Notary that Mr. Hanged Man had described. Then he headed once more for the twin towers north of the city to select a mystical item.

He hadn't done so earlier because his condition was unstable after advancing. He feared that contact with a mystical item might cause him to lose control.

Inside the spire, Derrick Berg listed his requirements after completing the formalities. He received a filtered list of items and the corresponding documents containing their information.

After careful reading and observation, he quickly narrowed his choices to two.

The first was Cardi's Ring, ancient and austere in appearance, entirely iron-black and engraved with dark, intricate patterns. It had been left behind by a City of Silver resident long ago. It could help the wearer overawe a target and make them halt. It could also temporarily deprive ordinary humans of reason or awaken the buried emotions and memories of a mad monster, confusing it briefly and preventing it from attacking.

The other item was Thunder God's Roar, recovered from a ruined city. It was extremely heavy and resembled a sledgehammer. Silver lightning wound around its dark blue surface, and gripping its handle felt like holding a creature's leg bone. In battle, it produced sounds that plunged enemies into fear and confusion, as though a Thunder God had descended and was roaring endlessly. Every strike carried immense destructive force and terrifying lightning.

Cardi's Ring had the negative effect of causing its wearer to unconsciously develop another "self." Therefore, the wearer needed periodic treatment from a Psyche Analyst. Missing any treatment could aggravate the condition and make it harder to cure. Eventually, the two selves would battle each other, ultimately causing the wearer to lose control.

Thunder God's Roar posed fewer hidden risks. It merely made its user increasingly irascible. As long as they regularly vented their emotions, there would be no problem. However, it had another side effect: in complete darkness, the chance of being attacked by terrifying monsters from the darkness's depths was one hundred percent!

The chance is one hundred percent... Derrick's mood darkened upon seeing the figure. This meant many City of Silver residents had vanished because of it.

They were Thunder God's Roar's former owners!

Which should I choose? Developing another "self" is terrifying. Miss Justice said it's called dissociative identity disorder... Thunder God's Roar can affect an enemy's condition and possesses considerable offensive power... My Axe of Hurricane has been used too often and will likely break prematurely... I can create light myself, so I don't need to fear absolute darkness... After a moment's thought, Derrick pointed at the dark blue sledgehammer.

"I want Thunder God's Roar."

At 160 Boklund Street, Klein left his master bedroom and saw Butler Walter waiting with several invitations.

"Sir, you have three invitations this week: afternoon tea on Wednesday, a literary salon on Friday, and a banquet on Saturday. They were sent by..."

Klein listened with a warm expression before smiling. "Tell these gracious hosts that I will attend."

"Very good, sir." Walter bowed and left the third floor.

Watching him disappear down the stairwell, Klein couldn't help sighing inwardly.

After another week of such invitations, it will be my turn to invite my neighbors to a banquet or ball...

Through this cycle of invitations and visits, I'll truly enter their circles and be recommended to more important figures at still higher levels. I'll gain entry to different clubs...

Heh heh, such recommendations are naturally founded on sufficient wealth. No one without value would be recommended...

High-society socializing is truly troublesome. It will take at least another month to contact anyone even slightly involved in the Great Smog of Backlund... The Church is easier. As long as I donate enough and appear sufficiently devout, I can freely come and go to hear the bishop's sermons. Of course, that's assuming I pass the investigation...

If everything proceeds smoothly, I'll determine the Keepers' rotation and find an opportunity to slip behind Chanis Gate...

Klein reined in his thoughts and instructed his valet, Richardson, to bring his coat, hat, and cane.

According to his schedule, he was going to the Royal Grand Theater to see the most popular recent play, The Betrayer's Ring.

This wasn't solely for entertainment. He needed to understand popular plays, famous music, and fashionable novels. Only then would he have common subjects to discuss at high-society gatherings.

Being a popular gentleman is exhausting. Behind one minute's performance lies ten years of effort... Social events truly are tiring... Lampooning inwardly, Klein let Richardson help him into his coat, then boarded his luxurious four-wheeled carriage. He went to the Royal Grand Theater in West Borough, occupied a private box, and watched The Betrayer's Ring.

It's different from television and film. Stage acting is more exaggerated and forceful. Yes, that's determined by the medium...

The story isn't bad, but why does it seem familiar? Don't tell me it was adapted from one of Emperor Roselle's works...

These are probably famous stage actors. The newspapers have mentioned them. They're said to be extremely popular, like celebrities on Earth during the Internet age...

As long as I attend high-society events, I'll certainly have opportunities to meet them... Klein watched the play while habitually lampooning inwardly.

At the ticket window of a large theater in Cherwood Borough, Melissa finally reached the front of the queue. She pushed banknotes and coins through the opening and said, "The Betrayer's Ring. Two tickets for three o'clock Sunday afternoon."

After watching The Betrayer's Ring, Klein sat in his carriage, sipping black tea as he accepted the evening papers Richardson had purchased.

He first turned to the theatrical reviews and read several professional critics' comments. Comparing their observations with his own, he gradually formulated a distinctive and profound interpretation.

Yes, at the very least, it's enough to fool those ladies and gentlemen... After finishing his "homework," Klein leisurely read the rest of the newspaper and was surprised by a report.

"The Backlund Bike Company's ten-percent stake has been sold. No further inquiries will be entertained!"

It's done? Publishing this report means Mr. Isengard has received the money... Klein was delighted at first, then frowned.

His gaze settled on the final exclamation mark!

The information was what he and Isengard Stanton had agreed upon beforehand, but the exclamation mark offended his eye.

That sentence doesn't require an exclamation mark... Mr. Isengard pays attention to detail. He wouldn't give the newspaper free rein... Did he deliberately leave the exclamation mark to send a message?

Is it a warning? While pondering, Klein suddenly realized something.

The ten-percent stake Isengard Stanton sold obviously belonged to Sherlock Moriarty. Given Isengard's character, he wouldn't sell it without reason. In a sense, that means Sherlock Moriarty or his agent has returned to Backlund!

Therefore, a faction of the royal family involved in the Great Smog of Backlund has keenly noticed the situation and begun monitoring Detective Isengard Stanton, hoping to capture whoever comes to collect the money!

How should I retrieve it... Klein appeared to read naturally as he seriously considered the problem.

Chapter 769: Sacrificing to Oneself

At ten that night, rain drizzled over Backlund once again. Beneath the streetlamps, a thin fog lent everything a blurred beauty.

Isengard Stanton's assistant, Bowen, inspected the ground floor one final time before approaching the oriel window to close the last open pane.

At that moment, a shadow darted inside and landed securely on the projecting wall.

It was a short-haired blue stray cat!

Seeing its large yellow eyes fixed upon him, Bowen couldn't help chuckling.

"There's no food here."

Because a detective's work made him vulnerable to reprisals and left him with many secrets to conceal, Isengard Stanton employed his cook and servants by the hour. They worked a fixed number of hours each day and never prepared excessive food, so dinner rarely left anything behind.

The blue cat opened its mouth, but instead of meowing, it spoke like a human. "I'm Sherlock Moriarty. I'm here to see Mr. Isengard Stanton."

"..."

Although Bowen was a Low-Sequence Beyonder nurtured by the Church of the God of Knowledge and Wisdom, his experience was limited. This was his first encounter with a talking cat, and he stood stunned and dazed.

After several seconds, he recovered and recalled what the cat had said.

It said... It said it's Sherlock Moriarty?

This great detective truly isn't simple!

He actually transformed into a cat--no, controlled one!

Such powers are exceedingly strange and terrifying!

Bowen quickly calmed down. Without answering the cat, he reached out and closed the window.

Afterward, he lowered his voice. "Follow me."

The cat immediately leaped down from the ledge behind the oriel window and agilely followed Bowen, tail raised. It went with him to the second floor and watched as he knocked on Isengard Stanton's bedroom door.

"Is something wrong?" Isengard asked, opening the door in faintly striped pajamas.

He had been enjoying his tobacco before bed.

Bowen cautiously pointed at the crouching blue cat beside him.

"Mr. Sherlock Moriarty is here to see you."

Isengard's eyebrows rose slightly above his thin face and white sideburns. Looking down, he stepped back twice and allowed the blue cat to saunter into his bedroom.

"Return to your room and sleep. Wake at the usual time tomorrow. We still have a case waiting," Isengard instructed Bowen as though nothing had happened.

After his assistant departed, he closed the door and turned toward the blue cat seated beside his reclining chair. He chuckled.

"I never expected you to possess such Beyonder powers. I was afraid you might come here directly."

"I noticed your exclamation mark," the cat replied with a smile.

It had to be said that such an expression looked extremely odd on a cat's face. Anyone who saw it would feel a chill run down their spine.

Isengard showed no unusual reaction. Drawing on his pipe, he sat in the reclining chair and slowly exhaled in comfort. Smiling, he said, "I trust your intelligence."

"Thank you for the compliment." The cat extended its paws and bowed politely.

Isengard observed it, rubbing his pipe as he smiled.

"You should understand what's happening.

"Those people don't dare watch me too closely. They fear I'll detect them and inform the Churches of Evernight and Steam. Heh heh, although exposure would be troublesome for them, I believe a demigod is among them. That is partly deduction and partly based on certain indications. After all, I've lived on this street for years.

"Therefore, neither people nor animals entering my house will be stopped. I believe you've already deduced that. But when you leave, you'll certainly be followed and tracked. Can you evade them? Hmm... The sum involved is considerable. Carrying it out would attract attention.

"Let me think. Did you come to ask me to deposit the money into a particular bank account, after which you'll have numerous people withdraw it from different parts of Backlund?"

Isengard smiled self-deprecatingly. "That's the best solution I can devise, but implementing it would be extremely troublesome."

The cat didn't answer directly, but gave a low chuckle.

"I only need you to lend me an empty room and three candles."

"No problem," Isengard said without pressing him. "The shares sold for 12,000 pounds. Hiring lawyers and accountants and publishing the advertisements cost 600 pounds. In addition, there was a stamp duty of 0.5 percent and a twenty-percent D-type tax. Ultimately, only 8,940 pounds remain."

D-type tax applied to commercial, financial, and professional income.

There's tax... More than 2,000 pounds vanished just like that... The cat's expression instantly froze.

As a Nighthawk, Klein's salary had been tax-exempt. Later, as a private detective, his income had been difficult to monitor, so he never declared it. After becoming an adventurer, he hadn't needed to pay tax because bounties on pirates received special concessions. Thus, he had never truly realized he needed to pay taxes. When Isengard Stanton mentioned them previously, he hadn't taken the matter seriously, believing the sum would be small. Reality had now dealt him a crushing blow.

No taxes had been levied on the previous share transaction because the Loen government exempted initial investments connected to inventions in order to encourage innovation.

After a brief silence, the cat's whiskers twitched. "All right, give me the cash. Eh, move it to the empty room.

"There's nothing wrong with the money, is there?"

"I've already checked it. They wouldn't play such a cheap trick. It would insult my intelligence." Isengard rose with his pipe. "Remember to mail me a signed confirmation letter afterward."

"It's already on the way," the cat replied, fully prepared.

Isengard opened the safe in the master bedroom with a key and combination. He removed stacks of banknotes and packed them into several briefcases.

Then he carried the cases out of the master bedroom and entered a guest room diagonally opposite.

"Check it." Isengard placed the cash-filled briefcases on the floor and addressed the cat that had followed him.

"I trust you," the cat said after glancing at them.

Isengard nodded and pointed to a cabinet.

"There are candles inside."

With that, he stepped out, held the doorknob, and smiled. "I'm very curious to see how you'll leave... I believe it will be an exciting magic show."

Isengard Stanton closed the door with a click, leaving the guest room silent and bleak.

Beside the cat, a figure appeared in a dark red coat and old tricorne hat. It was Klein's marionette, Wraith Senor.

He found the candles and quickly established a simple altar. Skipping many preliminary steps, he directly murmured in Elvish, "Blessed of the sea and spirit world, guardian of the Rorsted Archipelago, ruler of undersea creatures, master of tsunamis and storms, the great Kalvetua.

"Your devoted servant prays for your attention;

"I pray for you to accept his offerings.

"I pray for you to open the gates to your Kingdom."

The wind inside the wall of spirituality suddenly intensified. Senor quickly cut the back of his hand and scattered several drops of blood.

As a Sequence 5 Wraith, everything about him was rich in spirituality!

The fierce wind seized the blood and howled into the candle flame symbolizing Sea God Kalvetua. The flame swelled, forming an illusory door covered in mystical symbols and markings.

After about ten seconds, the door gave a heavy creak and slowly opened.

One by one, Senor lifted the briefcases of money and threw them through the illusory door.

When almost none remained, the gold coin stored within his body flew out and landed on the altar.

The Wraith's figure disappeared, projecting itself onto the coin's smooth face.

The gold coin trembled, rose into the air, hitched a ride on the last briefcase, and entered the illusory Door of Sacrifice.

Silently, the mysterious door closed, and the three candle flames returned to normal.

At that moment, the short-haired blue cat seemed to regain its senses. It looked around blankly and meowed.

After a while, Isengard opened the door and entered. All the briefcases and cash were gone. Only three candles burned silently while the short-haired blue cat arched its back at him warily.

As he examined the scene, a rental carriage slowly passed the fork at the street's far end.

That same night, in a building in Cherwood Borough, Xio returned home through the drizzle. Drying her hair with a towel, she told Fors, "Your letter has been mailed."

Fors acknowledged her tersely and began guessing when her teacher would reply.

At that moment, Xio lowered the towel and added, "There's news about Mr. X's gathering. It will be at the same place on Friday night."

Excellent. I can tell Mr. World! I wonder how much I'll need to pay... Fors's eyes brightened.

Before she could ask for details, Xio continued, "Mr. X also issued a mission said to carry a handsome reward. Eh, he wants information about people around us who possess abnormal luck."

"People with abnormal luck?" Fors murmured in puzzlement. "Is that man's brain functioning? Who would reveal information about people around them at such a gathering? It would make their true identity easy to discover."

"Who knows? Perhaps he truly is a lunatic." Xio knew no one with abnormal luck, so she answered casually.

Fors considered the matter carefully but couldn't discern the mission's true purpose. She could only set it aside. Once Xio went to bathe, she planned to pray to Mr. Fool and relay the relevant information to The World Gehrman Sparrow.

Chapter 770: A Child Should Act Like a Child

Mr. X is searching for people with abnormal luck? Above the gray fog, Klein pondered the information Miss Magician had provided, trying to extract something useful.

When his analysis yielded nothing, he approached the problem from another direction. He recalled the people around him with abnormal luck and searched for a connection.

Hmm... The Fog Sea's Strongest Hunter, Anderson Hood, counts as one... Dr. Aaron Ceres is another... Hmm, both were influenced by one of the Snakes of Fate... Angel of Fate Ouroboros was among Rose Redemption's founders. That secret organization supports and believes in the True Creator... The Aurora Order is effectively the True Creator's Church... A series of connections surfaced in Klein's mind, and he quickly reached a conclusion.

This is Angel of Fate Ouroboros's attempt to find Snake of Fate Will Auceptin!

"He" is driving the Aurora Order's members to help "Him" find Snake of Mercury Will Auceptin!

And that implies a King of Angels stands behind Mr. X somewhere in Backlund!

Under these circumstances, assassinating Mr. X would be tantamount to seeking death... No wonder he doesn't care about Backlund's peculiarities... The official factions would assume at most that a Saint supports him. Hmm, according to the official records, the Aurora Order has only five Saints. That would lead them to respond incorrectly... Having made his judgment, Klein's first thought was to reject Miss Magician's request and warn her not to provoke Mr. X.

If raising the alarm wouldn't affect Snake of Mercury Will Auceptin, and if the Church of the Evernight Goddess didn't clearly possess records of Dr. Aaron Ceres's bout of bad luck, Klein would have told Miss Magician to report both Angel of Fate Ouroboros and Mr. X to one of the Churches!

He calmly considered for several more seconds before conjuring The World Gehrman Sparrow and having him pray amid the gray fog.

"I'll confirm the situation and reply tomorrow."

He didn't reject the commission outright because he first planned to consult Snake of Mercury Will Auceptin!

He immediately returned to the real world, carefully removed the extremely fragile paper crane from his wallet, and gently unfolded it.

Klein didn't write at once. He first reviewed the questions he needed to ask Will. After composing a draft in his head, he took out a pencil and sharpened it with a blade.

After stretching his muscles, Klein wrote:

"Members of the Aurora Order are searching for people with abnormal luck.

"Do you know how to use a Worm of Time to create charms?

"Does your placental blood count as the blood of a Mythical Creature? If it does, I hope to obtain one drop. What price would I need to pay?"

Klein had originally planned to ask Will Auceptin how "He" preserved "His" rationality. After all, the Church's records indicated that no public faith in the Snake of Fate existed. Ultimately, however, he restrained himself, afraid that Will Auceptin in "His" infant state would reply, "What gave you the idea that I'm rational?"

Then Klein wouldn't know whether Will was joking or telling the truth.

Hmm, although no organization believes in the Snake of Fate, certain regions traditionally worship the God of Luck... Perhaps "They" are an alternate identity of Will Auceptin or Ouroboros... Klein muttered inwardly. Employing his Clown abilities to their fullest, he managed to refold the paper crane and place it beneath his pillow.

Only after all that did he have time to calculate his cash.

17,046 pounds, 5 gold coins, 3 soli, and 8 pence in change... If I also owned assets like houses, manors, and company shares, this much liquidity would make me quite a tycoon in Backlund... Of course, I'm still far from the greatest magnates. Their total assets must reach a million pounds... As Klein rejoiced in his considerable wealth, he recalled his debt and the large investments required to cultivate his persona.

He drank a mouthful of water, climbed into bed, and covered himself with a light but warm blanket before gradually falling asleep.

Amid his dreams, Klein abruptly became lucid and saw the desolate black plain.

He walked to the pitch-black steeple at its center, passed through the chaotic, unnatural layout, and entered the steeple's depths. As before, a circle of tarot cards lay on the floor.

However, no silvery writing covered the raised area at their center.

Will Auceptin didn't leave a reply... Then why did he draw me into this dream? Amid Klein's confusion, a black baby carriage suddenly rolled out of the shadows. Inside lay an infant wrapped in silver silk, its features indistinct!

"Mr. Snake of Fate?" Klein asked politely and cautiously.

The infant immediately replied in a clear voice, "What makes you so certain I'm a mister?"

Isn't that determined by your name? Don't fuss over such details! Klein lampooned inwardly, relaxing at the other's attitude.

"Then how should I address you?"

Will Auceptin, in infant form, answered tersely and with some perplexity, "I haven't decided...

"As you know--oh, you don't. Every time I begin again, I try to make myself slightly different to maintain a healthy mental state. A child should act like a child while they're still a child."

Klein's heart stirred.

"Is that how the Monster pathway preserves reason and resists madness?"

From the black carriage, Will Auceptin answered briskly, "Yes. Every beginning washes away the madness. However, I still need a certain amount of anchoring through faith. Otherwise, I couldn't maintain my Sequence 1 state for long.

"Heh heh, you're becoming more knowledgeable."

Oh, there are ways to resist madness besides anchoring oneself through faith. But "restarting" is clearly an ability of the Monster pathway's Sequence 1 Snake of Fate. Other pathways can't imitate it... Mr. Azik repeatedly loses his memories and finds himself anew. Does that involve the same principle? Klein nodded thoughtfully, making every second count. "I suspect Ouroboros is using Aurora Order members to search for you."

Will Auceptin scoffed.

"I've played hide-and-seek with 'Him' for a very long time. 'He' isn't good at it. It's obvious 'He' never had a childhood. Every time 'He' restarts, 'He' grows up beside the True Creator. 'He' lacks the psychological experience of life's different stages, so 'He' can be extremely mad at times. Of course, 'He' doesn't mind.

"I instructed Ricciardo to use the Die of Probability in certain places and leave traces. That will confuse Ouroboros's judgment. 'He' will leave Backlund again soon."

That means there's still a chance to assassinate Mr. X... Yes, when the time comes, I'll divine the danger above the gray fog... Klein didn't pursue the subject. "Do you know how to use a Worm of Time to create charms?"

Will Auceptin didn't answer directly. Instead, "He" asked, "You obtained a Worm of Time from Pallez Zoroast?"

"How did you know?" Klein asked in surprise.

He wasn't surprised that Will Auceptin could identify the Worm of Time's source--after all, few demigods of the Marauder pathway could create avatars--but why hadn't "He" assumed it came from Blasphemer Amon? The latter could also leave behind a Worm of Time!

Will Auceptin smiled. "Pallez Zoroast is in poor condition and had to parasitize your former colleague. Ah, yes, your former colleague was investigating Sherlock Moriarty and entered my house in the middle of the night.

"I sensed there was something wrong with him and gave him a brief spell of bad luck, causing him to encounter other demigods hidden in Backlund. When he was in danger, Pallez Zoroast acted. Haha, it wouldn't have mattered if he hadn't. It was only a prank. I would have given your former colleague enough good luck at the critical moment."

Leonard is investigating Sherlock Moriarty? The Grandpa inside him is called Pallez Zoroast... Klein frowned slightly, uncertain where the problem had arisen.

Will Auceptin continued, "Creating a Worm of Time charm isn't too difficult for you. You can pray to that unique trait surrounding you and use an amalgam of mercury and pure silver as the medium for drawing the corresponding symbols."

It isn't too difficult... Pray to The Fool? That's right. The mysterious space above the gray fog clearly exerts an attraction on the Marauder pathway... Klein was thrilled, feeling as though he had grasped something.

At that moment, Will smiled and added, "As for what the corresponding symbol is, I have no idea."

...What a reversal... The corner of Klein's mouth twitched.

When Will Auceptin fell silent, Klein hurriedly smiled. "There's one more question. About your placental blood..."

Before he could finish, Will suddenly opened "His" mouth and cried.

"Waaah!"

"He" began wailing like a real infant.

...Can't we have a normal conversation? Klein froze.

Had he not already confirmed it, he truly would have doubted whether the being before him was a Sequence 1 and the president of the Life School of Thought.

"All right, all right. I only wanted to ask whether it's the blood of a Mythical Creature." Klein raised his hands in surrender.

Will stopped crying and laughed. "Of course, but I'll exchange it beforehand. Otherwise, everyone would die on the spot."

"He" paused, then continued, "If you can give me something suitable, one drop isn't impossible.

"All right, goodbye!"

As soon as Will Auceptin said this, Klein felt the steeple tremble and the dream rapidly shatter.

He soon awakened.

Chapter 771: Fate Siphon

What item would interest a Snake of Fate? Klein slowly sat up and leaned against a pillow.

He thought for a while and decided to consider the matter later. After all, he was still at least a month away from Will Auceptin's birth. He could also leave the question for The Hermit Cattleya and Queen Mystic Bernadette, who was backing her, to rack their brains over.

Of course, Klein didn't eliminate the possibility of Will Auceptin's sudden decision to give birth early.

He slowly turned his attention to creating a Worm of Time charm. According to Will Auceptin's explanation, he had most of the conditions required, but he was just short of the corresponding symbol.

Pray to The Fool and use the powers of the mysterious space above the gray fog... I wonder if a symbol corresponding to the Marauder pathway would work... Even if it does, I don't know what it is. Unless I pull a Marauder above the gray fog and let the high-back chair produce the corresponding pattern... As Klein thought about the details, he suddenly had an idea.

In that case, perhaps he could try the symbol on the back of The Fool's chair!

It consisted of the Pupil-less Eye, which represented concealment, and the Contorted Lines, which represented change!

I wonder if it will work... Divination won't be able to rule it out by elimination, but I can predict if the attempt will be successful. Besides, even if it fails, it shouldn't be much of a problem. After all, I'm praying to myself. Even if the material were to be wasted in the experiment, it would enter above the gray fog and not be lost... With this in mind, Klein felt pumped. He couldn't help but get out of bed to try out the experiment that very night!

A material like the Worm of Time, left behind by a Marauder demigod at Amon's level, retains its essence and level even in death. When using it for a charm, it might not reach the level of an angel for various reasons, but it wouldn't be too far off. It should possess power near the peak of the Saint level. If Klein succeeded, it would be equivalent to having an additional trump card. At critical points in time, it might give him an additional life. How could he not be excited and full of anticipation?

I can only stir some of the powers of the mysterious space above the gray fog. The level of the Worm of Time charm will likely drop a little more. But regardless, it will definitely be like the Ninth Law given to me by Admiral Amurius... If I used the Blatherer's aura to create a high-level charm in the Devil domain, it will likely be at the level of the Ninth Law. Unfortunately, I wouldn't dare to pray to the Dark Side of the Universe... Clad in pajamas and standing barefoot on the thick carpet, Klein took four counterclockwise steps while chanting the incantation, then entered the space above the gray fog.

Sitting at The Fool's seat at the end of the long bronze table, he conjured a dark red fountain pen and yellowish-brown goatskin. He wrote down the corresponding divination statement:

"The charm I'm about to begin making will be successful."

Unwinding the spirit pendulum from his wrist, Klein held it with his left hand and entered the state of Cogitation.

After repeating the divination statement seven times, he opened his eyes and saw the topaz spinning counterclockwise rather slowly and through an ordinary arc.

This means that it will succeed... But that begs the question, will it successfully verify that the symbol I use is effective, or successfully verify that it doesn't work? As an experienced Seer, Klein attempted to interpret the revelation, but he failed to obtain any confirmation.

Faced with that ambiguity, he could only proceed with the experiment. There was no way to rule out possible errors without doing so.

Right on the heels of that, Klein wrote a new divination statement:

"The assassination of Mr. X this Friday will be dangerous."

This time, the topaz pendant continued spinning clockwise at a faster frequency and greater amplitude.

There's significant danger, but it doesn't reach the level of a demigod's participation, much less that of a King of Angels... If it involves an existence of that level, "He" will definitely sense my divination and resist it... From the looks of it, Angel of Fate Ouroboros will soon be led out of Backlund... This means that the danger itself is a result of Mr. X and his subordinates. It's within the limits of what I can handle... As long as I don't make a mistake, the chances of success are pretty high... Klein made a judgment, put down the pen and paper, and returned to the real world.

As a mysticism expert who often created charms, he had no lack of common materials. He immediately took out some candles and lit them on the table. Following that, he set up a simple altar against the glow of dusk. He then used a piece of silver to draw out the combined symbol that represented The Fool.

As Klein didn't know what Path Number The Fool represented or what magic labels there were, he could only ensure that both sides remained equal. According to the books of charms he had read, these would similarly satisfy the rules of mysticism, but the corresponding power would be reduced. The chances of failure would rise because the existence being prayed to might consider the petitioner irreverent and insufficiently devout. Of course, it wasn't a problem for Klein since he wouldn't reject himself.

After completing the act of carving the symbol, Klein found a metal bottle and used his spirituality to manipulate the container, pouring out the mercury to fill the carved-out pattern.

This time, he decided to complete only the front side for now. He would then summon and respond to himself. He would then bring the worm with the twelve translucent segments back to the room and then place it on a silver sheet.

After doing all of this, Klein adjusted the altar and took two steps back. He then said in ancient Hermes, "The Fool that doesn't belong to this era."

Following the process, he completed the necessary steps before taking four counterclockwise steps and entering the space above the gray fog. After imbuing himself with the Black Emperor card, he used his spirituality to stir a tiny amount of the powers above the gray fog to respond to his prayers.

While the surging energy poured into the circle of light, Klein didn't hesitate to return to the real world. He saw the altar had turned dark and gloomy, as though there were countless secrets hidden here. And the silver sheet had already floated up, fusing with the Worm of Time's corpse.

Klein took two steps forward, flipped the silver sheet over, and filled the carved symbol on the back with mercury.

As the lines lit up, they effused a hazy luster.

Klein rapidly retracted his arms and saw the luster grow richer. He then enveloped the silver sheet and the Worm of Time's corpse inside.

Suddenly, the darkness around the altar distorted as the entire space seemed to turn abnormal.

This change disappeared as fast as it came. The charm covered in strange patterns slowly landed on the desk. It was translucent and pitch-black. It was like a miniature card made out of a special crystal. It also resembled the eye of a particular existence that was watching this world.

It succeeded! It really works! Klein was delighted as he hurriedly picked up the charm. He found it cold to the touch as if he was touching snow.

Regardless of the charm's ultimate effects, its successful formation alone proved the experiment had worked!

Klein had once again obtained a high-level charm at the demigod level!

He busied himself again, bringing the completed item above the gray fog. He then used dream divination to determine how to use it.

The charm which was in the shape of a black crystal card had only one effect, but it was highly potent. It was to siphon off the luck of others. And to be precise, it was to graft fate--a period of the target's fate would be grafted onto the user!

In the simplest case, when an enemy is about to kill me, I'll use this charm, siphoning away his fate of survival, as well as grafting the fate of impending death onto him. Then, the situation would develop so that, despite clearly succeeding, he would be the one to die... It does match the Marauder pathway's usual traits, but it's more sinister and terrifying... It's going from stealing wealth to stealing fate... If the Worm of Time were alive, and I was able to fully employ the power of the mysterious space above the gray fog, this charm might even point towards the domain of time... As Klein thought, he felt a sense of fear.

If it wasn't for the assistance that this mysterious space had given him by obstructing and purging things, he had no way to deal with a Worm of Time!

Phew, now it's mine... I can't call it a Worm of Time anymore. I'll just call it Fate Siphon... Klein once again got busy as he brought back the Fate Siphon charm to the real world.

After dealing with the traces of the ritual, he gravely placed the high-level charm into the iron cigar case, putting it together with Azik's copper whistle and the Senor gold coin. He then sealed and isolated the case with a wall of spirituality.

Being in a good mood, Klein didn't feel sleepy. He parted the curtains slightly and allowed the crimson moonlight to shine in, filling his room with tranquil silence.

While he was enjoying the scenery, he suddenly saw a figure sneak out from Member of Parliament Macht's house as it approached amidst the shadows.

It was none other than Hazel Macht. She once again headed for the sewers, removed the manhole cover, climbed down, and didn't forget to close the cover.

Why is she always heading into the sewers? It's not likely for her to be heading to other areas from here to act like a superhero in the mysterious world. After all, each trip doesn't take her more than an hour. Unless she has very reliable intelligence, it's difficult to achieve anything. Besides, this will make it easy for her to be caught by the official Beyonders... Together with the scene provided to me by Arrodes, she's likely searching for something... Hmm, it's very easy for her to encounter danger if she keeps heading into the sewers... Klein stood behind the curtain gap as he observed what was happening under the serene night.

He didn't attempt to warn Hazel or let the Wraith possess her to let her understand the dangers of the Beyonder world. Firstly, he couldn't be certain that his subjective impression--that Hazel's sense of superiority resulted from her lack of mystical knowledge--was correct. Secondly, he wasn't sure how she had obtained Beyonder powers and a mystical item. Warning her out of gratitude for her kind deed earlier would easily attract unwanted attention or even trouble.

After enjoying an evening of peace, Klein returned to bed and slept until daybreak.

Before Richardson entered, he turned into Gehrman Sparrow and prayed to The Fool:

"I can accept the mission, but regardless of the outcome, I want a stone from that bracelet of yours, as well as the ability to use that spellbook of yours for some time.

"If it succeeds, all spoils of war will be mine. You can only take the target's head.

"If required, you will need to provide assistance."

Chapter 772: Walter's Abnormality

He wants a stone from my bracelet and the right to use Leymano's Travels for some time? How does he know that I have those two items? I don't remember mentioning it during the Tarot Gatherings... After hearing Gehrman Sparrow's response, Fors was bewildered and rather shocked. She felt as though he had seen through all her secrets.

She tensed up as she quickly tried to recall how this information could've been leaked.

Aside from Teacher, Xio, and Mr. Fool, no one knows that I have these two items, especially Leymano's Travels. I haven't even used it... Mr. Fool... Hmm, Mr. World has always seemed rather strange during the Tarot Gatherings; he never hands over Emperor Roselle's diary pages, and he doesn't seem to put any effort into this, nor does he show any concern... He and Mr. Fool have a deeper connection. He obtains the relevant information from "Him"? Is he a believer or a Blessed? Fors carefully thought over the matter as she grasped something, easing her horror from before.

Only at this moment did she have the time and energy to consider if she could accept Gehrman Sparrow's requests.

To Fors, the price was far too low, far lower than she had expected. Furthermore, it was reasonable!

As a Beyonder who seldom went out, preferring to stay home to write and rest, lending Leymano's Travels for some time didn't affect her safety or her need to use it. And likewise, giving up one of the two remaining stones in the bracelet that allowed her to travel through the spirit world wouldn't cost her every trump card.

The only problem is that Mr. World seems to be willing to only try it once. If he fails, he will still take the payment... Yes, with the fact that he needs to bear the risk, that's normal... I originally imagined that I would need to help him do many things and obtain a reward from Teacher by using the traitor's head to repay the debt... Fors thought calmly for a few seconds before she prayed to Mr. Fool:

"Please inform Mr. World that I accept his conditions, and I will try my best to provide him with assistance in the operation."

She had originally intended to warn Gehrman Sparrow that using the stone might result in the side effect of receiving the ravings of the full moon, but she then realized that it was apparently only something Beyonders from the Apprentice pathway encountered.

Regardless of whether it succeeds or fails, I'll obtain that stone. I'll be able to secretly leave Backlund and meet with Mr. Hanged Man to explore that primitive island... When the time comes, I'll use the spellbook to record the stone's power. That way, I don't have to worry about the return trip. That's unless my luck is terrible and the recording fails... Klein secretly heaved a sigh of relief, opened the door, and got Richardson to help him dress up.

"Sir, after breakfast, your schedule is to head to the Royal Museum to see the royal family's collection exhibition." As Richardson helped his employer wear his coat, he informed him of the day's schedule.

Since Dwayne Dantes had mastered social dancing very quickly, the number of etiquette classes in the morning went from five times a week to three times a week, allowing him to spare time for other things. And such exhibitions were definitely a hot topic of conversation in high society circles. Failing to attend in person would make him appear uninformed.

As for heading to Saint Samuel Cathedral for the bishop's sermons, Klein had consciously lowered his frequency. This wasn't because he needed to donate tens of pounds each time, but that he was afraid that heading there frequently after the novelty had worn off would incur suspicion. Appearing natural and reasonable was central to his plans.

Other than on a Sunday, he planned on randomly going to church on two of the remaining six days. He wanted to rely on an even longer period of time to accumulate intelligence so as to figure out a pattern. He couldn't be impatient or in a rush!

"I'm already looking forward to it." Klein looked at the dignified reflection of himself as he said to his valet with a smile.

Upon thinking of Saint Samuel Cathedral and the Church of Evernight, he naturally connected it to Leonard Mitchell's secret investigation of Sherlock Moriarty. He didn't understand what he was suspicious about.

Is it because of Emlyn White's purchase of Tinder that drove Leonard to investigate the people related to him, or was it because of the fleeting appearances of the detective in the cases of Capim and Lanevus that caused the Red Gloves in charge of the investigations to notice something? Or could it be both? Klein thought about the clues that he had left behind and had a rough guess.

He wasn't afraid that Sherlock Moriarty would be wanted by the Church of Evernight and given a bounty. After all, apart from contacting a few people that he was familiar with, the detective wasn't to appear again. He was worried that someone would discover that Sherlock Moriarty, in his early appearances, resembled Klein Moretti greatly, and as such, they would pursue the deceased former Nighthawk.

In fact, it's not a problem even if they discover that. I'm no longer the Clown or Magician from before. There are more than a handful of demigods searching for me. Even with the high-ranking deacons of the Church, there won't be any qualitative changes... Besides, Benson and Melissa truly are ordinary people. The Church will definitely not involve them and disturb their lives... I wonder if they will claim the bereavement compensation back. Probably not, for there's no way they can explain it to ordinary people... Klein wasn't overly worried despite all the trouble surrounding him.

This was also why he was so calm when he heard Will Auceptin mention Klein Moretti's identity last night.

How could a Sequence 1 Angel who was good at fate-related abilities and had previously interacted with Sherlock Moriarty early on not discover the detective's origins?

Even with the gray fog's obstruction that interfered with many details, Will Auceptin was definitely able to know that Sherlock Moriarty originally came from Tingen.

And back in Tingen, Klein had interacted with a youth named Ademisaul, who was of the Monster pathway, leading him to bleed from his eyes. And if Will Auceptin were to be aware of this and make a comparison, the answer was obvious.

If Leonard were to really realize Sherlock Moriarty's hidden identity, I wonder what kind of expression he would have... Klein gave a self-deprecating laugh as he walked out of the master bedroom. He went to the second floor to enjoy the breakfast his cook had prepared specially for him.

West Borough, 2 King's Avenue, Royal Museum.

Klein passed through the ticket entrance with Butler Walter and Valet Richardson and went into the museum.

The exhibition was held by the Loen royal family. They showcased all kinds of collections that had historical meaning from the kingdom's founding so as to allow the public to enjoy and gain an understanding. It was a way to raise the kingdom's citizens' respect and recognition of the royal family.

As a graduate from the Department of History, Klein was still rather interested in the exhibition. Many of the matters that he was very familiar with had corresponding artifacts on display here. They allowed him to plunge into the long and fascinating history from another angle.

What left Klein somewhat puzzled was Walter's deep knowledge of most of the exhibits. He introduced them to Dwayne Dantes in great detail.

As expected of a butler who came from an aristocratic household... Klein silently nodded.

As he perused the exhibits, the trio kept encountering other visitors, and the exhibition hall was quiet and orderly, so people had to converse in whispers.

When passing by an exhibit, Klein noticed Walter suddenly stop. He then glanced to his side as his expression turned complicated.

As he wasn't a Spectator, Klein wasn't able to interpret the actual meaning of those complicated feelings. All he could do was trace Walter's gaze towards the exhibit.

Standing in front of the exhibit were a man and a woman. The man was in his thirties and wore a black suit, silk hat, and a gold-inlaid cane, looking like a gentleman of status and wealth. The woman was in a yellow dress with a golden necklace. Her overall attire was inclined towards bright colors.

Mr. Butler is looking at that man... Klein instantly made the judgment as he swept his gaze past the target without anyone detecting him.

He realized that the man looked older than his years. His skin was dark as a result of frequent exposure to the sun. The back of his hand was like dried wood, and his fingers were extremely rough.

If I didn't look at his attire, I would've believed it if someone told me he was a farmer, gardener, or carriage driver... Klein retracted his gaze as he felt a little puzzled.

The reason why he noticed these details was because he had seriously considered the appearance of an ordinary person who adventured in the Southern Continent for extended periods of time back when he was constructing the identity of Dwayne Dantes.

He believed that apart from his gaze, bearing, and natural facial features that were etched by his rich experience, Dwayne Dantes also needed to have details, such as skin weathered by prolonged exposure to the sun, inconspicuous scars, and rough but strong palms. Otherwise, it wouldn't be enough to prop up such a character's essential traits.

I have to say that from the moment I became a Faceless, I'm getting more and more experienced and wise at creating new identities... If I were to return to Earth, even without my Beyonder powers, I'll have strong acting skills... As Klein made self-deprecating comments inwardly, he saw Walter recover from his stern look as though nothing had happened.

As for the man with somewhat old facial features and rough skin, he pointed at a flag inside the exhibition case.

"This is the flag that the Earl of Lastings, Prince Harrods Augustus had used during the White Rose War. Unfortunately, he perished in that war. However, his death was the turning point of the entire war and the reason why Loen eventually clinched victory. Look, the flag still has his blood..."

He's quite knowledgeable in the field of history... Klein's gaze swept toward Walter from the corner of his eye, thought for two seconds, and smiled. He approached the couple and interjected in a friendly manner:

"I never expected such a neglected tidbit of history would be known by someone else. I originally believed that the people's understanding of the White Rose War was only limited to Loen's victory against Intis.

"Sir, your eruditeness leaves me amazed."

To be praised in front of his female partner, the man's expression turned from a wary one to a relaxed one. A gleeful smile appeared on his face.

"I'm just a person who likes history."

He casually swept his gaze towards the servant of the gentleman in front of him as he suddenly frowned before easing his brows. A trace of puzzlement remained in his expression.

Indeed, he knows Butler Walter... Klein smiled while maintaining his composure.

"Hello there, I'm a merchant from Desi, Dwayne Dantes. How may I address you?"

The man hesitated and said, "William Sikes, a land steward at a manor."

Chapter 773: Additional Development

William Sikes... A land steward... Klein inwardly repeated the response he got before turning the conversation to the flag and the White Rose War.

After a short chat, he politely bade farewell and walked towards the other exhibits with Walter and Richardson. He continued his own tour of the exhibits, as though his encounter from before was completely trivial, a purely coincidental conversation.

When it was almost noon, Klein, who had returned to his high-end four-wheeled carriage, watched the passing bicycles through the window before suddenly saying, "Walter, you seem to know Mr. William Sikes?"

Walter solemnly nodded and said, "I once knew him while I was working for Viscount Conrad's household.

"He served a member of the royal family, the former Earl of Lastings, Prince Edessak."

He didn't conceal anything, and he described William Sikes's background in detail.

He was once in service of Prince Edessak? He seems to be living quite well after the prince passed away because of the Great Smog of Backlund. I wonder what manor he's the land steward of... Perhaps he knows some secrets? Klein gently nodded and didn't probe further. He was wondering if he should find an opportunity to investigate William Sikes.

If William Sikes really knows something, the royal family's faction wouldn't leave him be. Or perhaps he is part of that faction. In short, investigating him will be a rather dangerous matter. There's no way to entrust this matter to Miss Magician, Emlyn White, or Miss Xio... Miss Sharron has the ability to do so, but this might result in destroying her peaceful life... The best solution is still to use Hero Bandit Black Emperor. But the problem is that before stealing the Antigonus family's notebook, my investigations of the Great Smog of Backlund should only be superficial. I shouldn't alarm anyone or bring about any accidental changes... Klein appeared to admire the streets outside, but many thoughts were going through his mind.

Finally, he decided to hold back for the time being, being unwilling to affect the most pressing matter at hand.

After having lunch and taking a nap, Klein attended a lesson in literary appreciation until it was almost evening.

After sending away his teacher, he was just about to head to the second-floor dining room when he suddenly heard the doorbell ring.

Amidst the ringing, Klein saw Richardson immediately take a few steps forward to open the door.

Standing outside were two police officers in black-and-white checkered uniforms. From their epaulets, one of them was a high-ranking inspector, while the other was a sergeant.

"Officers, how may I help you?" Richardson asked on behalf of his employer.

The high-ranking inspector was a thin man and had his black hair hidden beneath his peaked cap. His sideburns were faintly graying as he swept his gaze into the house before warmly saying with a smile, "I'm here for Mr. Dwayne Dantes. There's a case that involves him and his butler."

"What is it?" Klein slowly walked to the door. "I'm Dwayne Dantes."

After introducing himself, he asked politely, "Officers, how may I address you?

"If the matter is a little more complicated and will take some time, why not come to my parlor. We can discuss it over tea."

The other police officer, the sergeant, was an elegant woman. She was clearly interested in taking up the offer as she looked at the high-ranking inspector, awaiting the decision of her superior.

Due to the Church of the Evernight Goddess, the Loen police force had plenty of female officers, but due to the other faiths and the prevailing trends of society, they suffered some form of discrimination when it came to promotions and positions. They mostly did clerical work, and there was an invisible ceiling for their career development.

The high-ranking inspector smiled and said, "There's no need for tea, but we need to question your household staff."

He paused before getting to the main point.

"Mr. Dwayne Dantes, do you know a person by the name of William Sikes?"

"I got to know him this morning at the Royal Museum." Klein vaguely sensed that some sort of unexpected development had occurred as he asked, "Did something happen to him?"

I just met him, and he's dead?

Had he already been targeted?

Trissy Cheek!

Chapter 774: Clues

She isn't dead? She managed to escape? She's actually trying to seek revenge for Prince Edessak? The moment he saw Trissy, Klein nearly lost control of his expression.

She had stolen the Beyonder power, Wraith's Shriek!

Hazel still has the basic presence of mind to react. She's not a complete newbie... In the sewers, Senor appeared with his dark red coat and triangular hat as he spoke silently.

No way, she really seems to mind Prince Edessak's death... Lady, have you forgotten that you were once a man? Have you forgotten the pain you were previously talking about? You're more talented than I expected... It's not easy being a mirror these days... However, why does that final statement sound odd... Klein's facial muscles trembled as he said, "When dealing with them, hide the nature of the question-and-answer game. Don't let others notice it."

Chapter 777: Sick and Crazy Setup

On Friday afternoon, Klein, who had skipped a literary salon because of his feigned sickness, arrived above the gray fog once again.

As he injected some of his spirituality into Leymano's Travels, making it light up with a faint luster, he made the blue gem on the scepter's tip emit a bright light.

A sizzling sound was produced as bolts of silver lightning appeared out of thin air. They were thick and menacing as they crackled wildly, meshing together to form a hurricane.

It failed again... Klein sighed silently as he repeated the same process.

During this process, there were successes and failures. Klein relied on repeated attempts before finally succeeding, recording the Lightning Storm power which he had coveted for a very long time!

This spellbook isn't very useful to unaffiliated Beyonders. It needs plenty of time and patience to record a sufficient number of powers. In the East Borough, inside a cheap motel, Klein entered the room that Miss Magician had reserved for him under a fake alias.

Chapter 778: 1 + 1 > 2

While passing through the living room, Klein's spiritual intuition was triggered. He felt that there was an invisible gaze scanning him from somewhere unknown.

Admiral of Blood Senor!

A Wraith's possession!

This was the second most difficult part of his plan.

To silently control Mr. X and remove his ability to resist wasn't difficult with the combination of Marionettist and Wraith. Killing him wasn't difficult either. Mr. X in his present state could easily be finished off by Klein with an Air Bullet.

Chapter 779: One-Shot

The three types of paper in Leymano's Travels clearly had different textures. The white paper that could only record Sequence 7, 8, and 9 powers was thin, smooth, and flat. The yellowish-brown goatskin pages, which could record Sequence 5 and 6 powers, were as pliable as tanned leather. The three charred yellow pages that could record godhood powers were thick and textured. Together, they allowed someone to quickly distinguish between them simply from touch.

Klein's fingers quickly found the three thick, textured pages as he gently pinched the middle page.

Although his pocket wasn't large enough, preventing him from fully opening Leymano's Travels, the hooded robe was personally modified by Fors herself. The pocket had plenty of space, allowing the palm-sized spellbook to be flipped to a right angle.

As Klein used his palm to prevent Leymano's Travels from closing, he used his finger to slide across the corresponding page's surface. The surface had slight bumps and depressions, making the strange patterns and symbols that were filled with mystery and an ancient aura be directly presented in his mind.

He injected his spirituality into it.

This charred-yellow page recorded a demigod power of the Sailor pathway: Hurricane!

Klein wanted to use it to create chaos, so as to interfere with the Aurora Order Saint who was lurking in the darkness. By doing so, he could seize the opportunity to assassinate Mr. X and escape with the help of the wind.

Groselle's Travels!
